a producer of programmes = 节目制作人

product
/ˈprɒdʌkt/
核心：名词：产品、制品：生产出来卖的东西，可数也可不可数；（自然过程、化学反应的）产物，可数；
（某种环境、经历造就的）结果、产物：the product of，可数；（数学）乘积，可数。
① The firm launched six new products at the trade fair.
= 那家公司在交易会上推出了六种新产品。
a new product = 新产品
② The shop sells dairy products from farms within ten miles.
= 那家店卖十英里以内农场出的乳制品。
dairy products = 乳制品
③ The gas is a waste product of the process.
= 那种气体是这道工序的废弃产物。
a waste product = 废弃产物
④ Her confidence is the product of a happy childhood.
= 她的自信是幸福童年的结果。
the product of something = 某事的结果
⑤ The product of six and seven is forty-two.
= 六和七的乘积是四十二。
the product of six and seven = 六乘七的积
⑥ The film is full of product placement, with the same watch in almost every scene.
= 那部片子里到处是植入广告，同一块表几乎每场戏都出现。
product placement = 植入广告
厂商付钱让产品出现在影视里。

production
/prəˈdʌkʃn/
核心：名词：生产、制造、产量，不可数：in production、go into production；（自然的）产生、分泌，不可数；
（电影、戏剧、节目的）制作，不可数，也指上演的一出戏、摄制的一部片子，可数。
习语：on production of 是（正式）出示……后；make a production of 是把一件小事弄得小题大做。
复合词：production line 是生产线；production values 是（电影、演出的）制作水准；production number 是（歌舞片里场面大的）歌舞段落。并入本条。
① The production of steel has fallen by a third in ten years.
= 钢产量十年里下降了三分之一。
the production of steel = 钢的生产
② The new model will go into production early next year.
= 新型号明年初投产。
go into production = 投产
in production = 在生产中
③ The drug slows the production of acid in the stomach.
= 那种药减缓胃酸的分泌。
the production of acid = 酸的分泌
④ The school's production of the comedy sold out on all three nights.
= 学校排的那出喜剧三个晚上的票都卖光了。
a production of a play = 一出戏的演出
⑤ Discounts are given on production of a student card.
= 出示学生证可以打折。
on production of = 出示……后
on production of something = 出示某物后
⑥ He makes a production of every small repair in the house.
= 家里每一处小修小补，他都弄得兴师动众。
make a production of something = 把某事弄得小题大做
⑦ She worked on the production line for thirty years.
= 她在生产线上干了三十年。
a production line = 生产线
⑧ The film has a weak story but very high production values.
= 那部片子故事薄弱，制作水准却很高。
production values = 制作水准
⑨ The show ends with a big production number for the whole cast.
= 那场演出以全体演员的一大段歌舞收尾。
a production number = 大场面的歌舞段落
⑩ The film spent two years in pre-production before a single scene was shot.
= 那部片子光前期筹备就花了两年，一场戏都还没拍。
pre-production = （影视的）前期筹备
拍完之后的剪辑、配音叫 post-production。

productive
/prəˈdʌktɪv/
核心：三支。一是多产的、产量高的、效率高的；二是富有成效的、有收获的；三是（正式）productive of：产生、引起（某种结果）的。
副词 productively。反义是 unproductive。并入本条。
① The land in the valley is the most productive in the region.
= 山谷里的地是那个地区产量最高的。
productive land = 产量高的地
② We had a very productive meeting and settled all four questions.
= 我们开了一个很有成效的会，把四个问题都解决了。
a productive meeting = 有成效的会
③ The long delay was productive of nothing but bad temper.
= 那次长时间的延误除了让人生气，什么也没带来。
productive of something = 产生某事
④ She learned to use the time on the train productively.
= 她学会了把火车上的时间用得有成效。
productively = 有成效地

productivity
/ˌprɒdʌkˈtɪvəti/
核心：生产率、生产效率：一定的时间、人力里能生产出多少。不可数。
① The new machines have raised productivity by a fifth.
= 新机器把生产率提高了五分之一。
raise productivity = 提高生产率
② Wages can rise only if productivity improves as well.
= 只有生产率也提高，工资才能涨。
productivity improves = 生产率提高

prof
/prɒf/
核心：（口语）教授：professor 的截短。可数。写在姓名前的 Prof. 是 Professor 的缩写。
① The old prof lectured for an hour without looking at his notes.
= 老教授讲了一个钟头课，没看一眼讲稿。
a prof = 教授
② The letter was signed by Prof. Jones of the history department.
= 那封信的落款是历史系的琼斯教授。
Prof. Jones = 琼斯教授

profane
/prəˈfeɪn/
核心：形容词：亵渎神明的、不敬的；（语言）粗俗下流的；（正式）世俗的、非宗教的，跟 sacred 相对。动词：（正式）亵渎、玷污。
名词 profanity 是亵渎、不敬，不可数；也指咒骂的话、脏话，可数，多用复数。并入本条。
① The priest complained about the profane language of the workmen in the church.
= 神父抱怨工人们在教堂里说粗话。
profane language = 粗话
② The concert mixed sacred music with profane songs about love and wine.
= 那场音乐会把宗教音乐和歌唱爱情美酒的世俗歌曲掺在一起。
profane songs = 世俗歌曲
③ The soldiers profaned the temple by stabling their horses in it.
= 士兵们把马拴在庙里，亵渎了那座庙。
profane a temple = 亵渎庙宇
④ He let out a string of profanities when the hammer hit his thumb.
= 锤子砸到拇指，他骂出一串脏话。
profanities = 脏话
profanity = 亵渎、不敬

profess
/prəˈfes/
核心：（正式）动词，及物：自称、声称（往往不是真的）：profess to do、profess ignorance；公开表明、承认（感情、信念）；信奉（某种宗教）。
分词 professed 作形容词，只作定语：自称的、公开承认的。并入本条。
① He professed to know nothing about the missing money.
= 他声称对那笔不见了的钱一无所知。
profess to do something = 声称做某事
② She professed herself satisfied with the result of the inquiry.
= 她表示对调查的结果满意。
profess yourself satisfied = 表示满意
③ The villagers still profess the faith of their grandparents.
= 村民们仍信奉祖辈的信仰。
profess a faith = 信奉一种信仰
④ He is a professed enemy of every kind of modern music.
= 他公开宣称自己跟一切现代音乐势不两立。
a professed enemy = 公开的敌人

profession
/prəˈfeʃn/
核心：名词：（需要专门训练的）职业：尤指医生、律师、教师这一类，可数：by profession 是论职业；the profession 是全体同行、业界，单数，动词单复数都行；
（正式）（感情、信仰的）表白、声明：a profession of，可数。
① She is a lawyer by profession and a painter in her spare time.
= 她的职业是律师，业余画画。
by profession = 论职业
② He decided to enter the teaching profession at the age of forty.
= 他四十岁决定进教师这一行。
the teaching profession = 教师这一行
③ The report was welcomed by the medical profession.
= 那份报告受到医学界的欢迎。
the medical profession = 医学界
④ His professions of friendship did not stop him from selling the story.
= 他口口声声说是朋友，却照样把那件事卖给了报纸。
a profession of friendship = 友谊的表白

professional
/prəˈfeʃənl/
核心：形容词：职业的、专业上的，只作定语：professional advice；（做事）内行的、够专业水准的；以此为职业的、职业选手的，跟 amateur 相对。
名词，可数：专业人员：医生、律师一类；职业选手、以此为业的人；内行、行家。副词 professionally。
派生：professionalism 是专业水准、职业操守，不可数；professionalize 是使职业化，名词 professionalization。并入本条。
① You should take professional advice before you sign the contract.
= 签合同之前，你应当请教专业人士。
professional advice = 专业意见
② The builders did a very professional job on the roof.
= 建筑工把屋顶修得很够专业水准。
a professional job = 够专业水准的活
③ He became a professional footballer at the age of seventeen.
= 他十七岁成了职业足球运动员。
a professional footballer = 职业足球运动员
④ The clinic is staffed by doctors, nurses and other health professionals.
= 那家诊所有医生、护士和别的医疗专业人员。
health professionals = 医疗专业人员
⑤ The race is open to both amateurs and professionals.
= 那场比赛业余选手和职业选手都可以参加。
a professional = 职业选手
⑥ She sings professionally with a small opera company.
= 她在一家小歌剧团当职业歌手。
professionally = 作为职业
⑦ The nurses were praised for their professionalism during the emergency.
= 护士们在那次紧急情况中的专业表现受到称赞。
professionalism = 专业水准、职业操守
⑧ The sport was professionalized in the nineteen nineties.
= 那项运动是一九九〇年代职业化的。
professionalize = 使职业化
professionalization = 职业化
⑨ The defender was sent off for a professional foul on the edge of the penalty area.
= 那名后卫在禁区边上故意犯规，被罚下了场。
a professional foul = 故意犯规
为了不让对方得分而存心犯的规。

professor
/prəˈfesə(r)/
核心：教授：英国大学里是一个系里级别最高的教师；美国大学里泛指大学教师，分 assistant professor（助理教授）、associate professor（副教授）、full professor（正教授）。可数；用在姓名前大写。
派生：professorial 是教授的、教授派头的；professorship 是教授的职位。并入本条。
① She was made a professor of history at the age of thirty-five.
= 她三十五岁当上了历史学教授。
a professor of history = 历史学教授
② My chemistry professor at college never remembered anyone's name.
= 我大学的化学老师从来记不住谁的名字。
a college professor = 大学教师
③ He peered at us over his glasses in a professorial manner.
= 他从眼镜上方打量我们，一副教授派头。
professorial = 教授派头的
④ She was offered a professorship at a university in Canada.
= 加拿大的一所大学请她去当教授。
a professorship = 教授职位

proffer
/ˈprɒfə(r)/
核心：（正式）两支，及物。一是递上、端给：proffer something to somebody；二是提出（建议、解释、帮助）。
① The waiter proffered a tray of drinks to each guest as they came in.
= 客人进门时，服务员向每位递上一托盘饮料。
proffer something to somebody = 把某物递给某人
② She took the proffered hand and shook it warmly.
= 她握住伸过来的那只手，热情地摇了摇。
the proffered hand = 伸过来的手
③ He proffered no explanation for arriving three hours late.
= 迟到三个钟头，他没作任何解释。
proffer an explanation = 作出解释

proficient
/prəˈfɪʃnt/
核心：熟练的、精通的：因为练得多而做得好：proficient in、at。名词 proficiency，不可数。并入本条。
① She is proficient in three languages and can read two more.
= 她精通三种语言，另有两种能阅读。
proficient in something = 精通某事
② He soon became proficient at mending the engine himself.
= 他很快就能熟练地自己修发动机了。
proficient at doing something = 做某事熟练
③ Applicants must pass a test of proficiency in English.
= 申请人须通过英语水平测试。
proficiency in something = 某方面的熟练程度

profile
/ˈprəʊfaɪl/
核心：名词，可数：（人脸的）侧面、侧影：in profile 是从侧面；（人物、机构的）简介、概况：报刊上的一篇人物特写，网上的个人资料；
（在公众面前的）形象、受注意的程度：raise the profile of；（物体的）外形、轮廓。
动词：写……的简介、扼要介绍。
习语：keep a low profile 是保持低调、不惹人注意；a high profile 是高曝光度，形容词 high-profile 是引人注目的。
profiling 是（根据特征）剖析、画像：给嫌疑人、顾客归类；做这事的人叫 profiler。并入本条。
① The coin shows the queen's head in profile.
= 那枚硬币上是女王头像的侧面。
in profile = 从侧面
② The magazine printed a profile of the new head of the bank.
= 那本杂志登了一篇新任行长的人物特写。
a profile of somebody = 某人的简介
③ She has not updated her profile on the website for three years.
= 她在那个网站上的个人资料三年没更新了。
a profile = 个人资料
④ The campaign has raised the profile of the disease among young people.
= 那场宣传让那种病在年轻人当中受到了更多关注。
raise the profile of something = 提高某事的关注度
⑤ The car has a low profile that helps it to cut through the wind.
= 那辆车外形低矮，有助于破风。
a low profile = 低矮的外形
⑥ The programme profiles a different scientist every week.
= 那个节目每星期介绍一位不同的科学家。
profile somebody = 介绍某人
⑦ He has kept a low profile since the scandal and rarely leaves the house.
= 丑闻之后他一直保持低调，很少出门。
a low profile = 低调
keep a low profile = 保持低调
⑧ The trial has a high profile because the accused is a famous actor.
= 那场审判备受关注，因为被告是位名演员。
high profile = 高曝光度
⑨ The police used profiling to narrow the list of suspects to three men.
= 警方用特征剖析把嫌疑人名单缩到了三个人。
profiling = 特征剖析
a profiler = 做特征剖析的人

profit
/ˈprɒfɪt/
核心：名词：利润、盈利：赚的钱超过花的钱的那部分，可数也可不可数：make a profit；（正式）好处、益处，不可数。
动词：（正式）得益、获利：profit from、by；对……有好处。
习语 at a profit 是赚了钱地、高于成本地。
派生与复合词：profitable 是有利润的、有益的，副词 profitably，名词 profitability；profitless 是无利可图的、无益的；
profit margin 是利润率；profit-sharing 是分红制；profit-taking 是（股市上）获利回吐；profit warning 是盈利预警。并入本条。
① The firm made a profit of two million pounds last year.
= 那家公司去年盈利两百万英镑。
make a profit = 盈利
② There is little profit in arguing with a man who will not listen.
= 跟一个不肯听的人争，没什么好处。
profit in doing something = 做某事的好处
③ She sold the house at a profit two years after she bought it.
= 买下那座房子两年后，她赚了钱把它卖了。
at a profit = 赚了钱地
④ Small farmers have not profited from the rise in food prices.
= 小农户没有从粮价上涨里得到好处。
profit from = 从……得益
profit from something = 从某事得益
⑤ The shop became profitable in its third year.
= 那家店第三年开始赚钱。
profitable = 有利润的
profitably = 有利润地
⑥ The bank is worried about the profitability of its branches in small towns.
= 那家银行担心小城镇分行的盈利能力。
profitability = 盈利能力
⑦ It was a profitless argument that left everyone tired and angry.
= 那是一场毫无益处的争论，弄得人人又累又气。
profitless = 无益的、无利可图的
⑧ The supermarkets work on a very small profit margin.
= 超市的利润率很低。
a profit margin = 利润率
⑨ The firm runs a profit-sharing scheme for all its staff.
= 那家公司对全体员工实行分红制。
profit-sharing = 分红制
⑩ Shares fell slightly as a result of profit-taking after a week of gains.
= 涨了一个星期之后，有人获利回吐，股价略有下跌。
profit-taking = 获利回吐
⑪ The company's shares dropped by a fifth after it issued a profit warning.
= 那家公司发出盈利预警后，股价跌了五分之一。
a profit warning = 盈利预警
⑫ The accountant asked to see the profit and loss account for the last three years.
= 会计要看近三年的损益表。
a profit and loss account = 损益表
列出一段时间里的收入、开支和盈亏。
⑬ The railway has never been a profit-making business and depends on public money.
= 那条铁路从来不是赚钱的买卖，靠公家的钱维持。
profit-making = 赚钱的、营利的
反过来是 non-profit-making（非营利的）。

profiteer
/ˌprɒfɪˈtɪə(r)/
核心：（贬）名词：牟取暴利的人、奸商：趁战争、短缺的时候抬价发财的人，可数。动词：牟取暴利。这种行为叫 profiteering，不可数。并入本条。
① The profiteers sold bread at ten times its normal price during the siege.
= 围城期间，奸商把面包卖到平时十倍的价钱。
a profiteer = 奸商
② The firm was accused of profiteering from the shortage of medicines.
= 那家公司被指责趁药品短缺牟取暴利。
profiteer from something = 趁某事牟取暴利
③ The government passed a law against profiteering in wartime.
= 政府通过了一项禁止战时牟取暴利的法律。
profiteering = 牟取暴利

profiterole
/prəˈfɪtərəʊl/
核心：泡芙、奶油小空心饼：一种小圆酥皮点心，里面填奶油，上面浇巧克力酱。可数。
① She ordered profiteroles with hot chocolate sauce for pudding.
= 甜点她点了浇热巧克力酱的泡芙。
profiteroles = 泡芙
② The wedding cake was a tower of profiteroles held together with caramel.
= 那个婚礼蛋糕是用焦糖粘起来的一座泡芙塔。
a tower of profiteroles = 泡芙塔

profligate
/ˈprɒflɪɡət/
核心：（正式，贬）挥霍无度的、恣意浪费的：花钱、用东西毫无节制；也指放荡的。名词 profligacy，不可数。并入本条。
① The report attacks the profligate spending of the last government.
= 那份报告抨击上届政府挥霍无度的开支。
profligate spending = 挥霍无度的开支
② We cannot go on being so profligate with water in a dry country.
= 在一个干旱的国家，我们不能再这样浪费水了。
profligate with something = 对某物浪费无度
③ The profligacy of the young king emptied the treasury in five years.
= 年轻的国王挥霍无度，五年就把国库花空了。
profligacy = 挥霍无度

profound
/prəˈfaʊnd/
核心：三支。一是（感受、影响）极大的、深切的、深远的；二是（见解、学问）深刻的、渊博的、有深度的；三是（医学、正式）严重的、彻底的：profound deafness。
副词 profoundly；名词 profundity 是深刻、深度，不可数，也指深奥的话，可数。并入本条。
① The death of his father had a profound effect on the boy.
= 父亲的去世对那男孩影响极深。
a profound effect = 极深的影响
② The book offers a profound understanding of how children learn.
= 那本书对孩子怎样学习有深刻的理解。
a profound understanding = 深刻的理解
③ The child was born with a profound loss of hearing in both ears.
= 那孩子生下来两只耳朵就重度失聪。
a profound loss of hearing = 重度失聪
④ I was profoundly moved by the letters that the soldiers wrote home.
= 士兵们写回家的那些信深深打动了我。
profoundly = 深深地
⑤ Readers admire the profundity of her last essays.
= 读者钦佩她最后几篇文章的深度。
profundity = 深刻、深度

profuse
/prəˈfjuːs/
核心：大量的、极其丰富的、源源不断的：多说流血、出汗、道歉、道谢。副词 profusely，并入本条。
① The patient was brought in with profuse bleeding from a cut on the head.
= 那位病人送来时，头上的一道伤口大量出血。
profuse bleeding = 大量出血
② She offered profuse apologies for keeping us waiting in the rain.
= 让我们在雨里等，她一再道歉。
profuse apologies = 一再的道歉
③ He was sweating profusely after carrying the piano up three floors.
= 把钢琴抬上三楼以后，他大汗淋漓。
sweat profusely = 大汗淋漓

profusion
/prəˈfjuːʒn/
核心：（正式，文学）大量、丰富、充沛。单数或不可数：a profusion of、in profusion。
① Roses grow in profusion along the south wall of the garden.
= 花园的南墙边玫瑰开得很盛。
in profusion = 大量地
② The market offers a profusion of fruit that I had never seen before.
= 那个集市上有大量我从没见过的水果。
a profusion of something = 大量的某物

progenitor
/prəʊˈdʒenɪtə(r)/
核心：（正式）两支，可数。一是（人、动植物的）祖先、始祖；二是（一种思想、一场运动的）先驱、创始人。
① The wild horse of the steppes is the progenitor of every modern breed.
= 草原上的野马是所有现代马种的祖先。
the progenitor of something = 某物的祖先
a progenitor = 祖先、始祖
② She is regarded as the progenitor of the modern detective novel.
= 她被看作现代侦探小说的开山鼻祖。
the progenitor of a movement = 一场运动的创始人
多指开创者，口气比 founder 书面。

progeny
/ˈprɒdʒəni/
核心：（正式，或玩笑）子女、后代、（动植物的）幼仔、后裔。复数名词。别跟 prodigy（神童）混。
① He appeared at the gate with his wife and all six of his progeny.
= 他带着妻子和六个子女出现在院门口。
his progeny = 他的子女
这样用多带玩笑口气。
② The progeny of the two plants had flowers of a quite different colour.
= 那两株植物的后代开的花颜色完全不同。
the progeny of something = 某物的后代
produce progeny = 繁殖后代

progesterone
/prəˈdʒestərəʊn/
核心：孕酮、黄体酮：女性、雌性动物体内的一种激素，让子宫为怀孕作好准备。不可数。
① The level of progesterone in the blood rises sharply after the egg is released.
= 排卵之后，血液里孕酮的水平急剧上升。
the level of progesterone = 孕酮的水平
progesterone = 孕酮、黄体酮
② The pill contains a small dose of progesterone and no other hormone.
= 那种药片含小剂量的孕酮，不含别的激素。
a dose of progesterone = 一剂孕酮
常跟 oestrogen（雌激素）并提。

prognosis
/prɒɡˈnəʊsɪs/
核心：两支，可数，复数 prognoses。一是（医学）预后：医生对病情会怎样发展的判断；二是（正式）（对事情前景的）预测。
形容词 prognostic；prognostication 是（正式）预言、预测，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① The doctors say that the prognosis is good if the growth is removed at once.
= 医生说，那个肿物马上切除的话，预后良好。
the prognosis is good = 预后良好
a poor prognosis = 预后不良
② The bank gave a gloomy prognosis for the economy next year.
= 那家银行对明年的经济作了悲观的预测。
a prognosis for something = 对某事前景的预测
a gloomy prognosis = 悲观的预测
③ The size of the tumour is an important prognostic factor.
= 肿瘤的大小是重要的预后因素。
prognostic = 预后的
a prognostic factor = 预后因素
④ None of the experts' prognostications about the election came true.
= 专家们对那场选举的预言一个也没应验。
a prognostication = 预言
口气略带嘲讽：说得煞有介事的预言。
别跟 diagnosis 混：diagnosis 是「得的是什么病」，prognosis 是「往后会怎样」。

programme
/ˈprəʊɡræm/
核心：名词，可数：（电视、广播的）节目；计划、方案、规划：一系列有安排的活动：a training programme、a programme of reform；
（演出、典礼的）节目单、程序册；课程、大纲；（机器按步骤运行的）程序：a wash programme。
动词：为……安排（时间、活动），多用被动；预先设定（机器）让它自动做：programme something to do。
美式拼作 program。电脑的「程序」和「编程」英美都拼 program，过去式 programmed，美式也作 programed。
派生：programmer 是程序员；programming 是编程，也指（电台、电视台的）节目编排；programmable 是可编程的；
programmatic 是（正式）纲领性的、按计划的，副词 programmatically，在计算机里指通过程序地。并入本条。
① We watched a programme about wolves on television last night.
= 昨晚我们在电视上看了一个讲狼的节目。
a programme about something = 关于某事的节目
a television programme = 电视节目
② The firm runs a training programme for school leavers every autumn.
= 那家公司每年秋天给中学毕业生办培训计划。
a training programme = 培训计划
run a programme = 办一个计划
③ The government has announced a programme of reform for the railways.
= 政府宣布了一套铁路改革方案。
a programme of reform = 改革方案
a programme of something = 一套……的方案
④ I bought a programme at the door to see who was singing.
= 我在门口买了份节目单，看都有谁唱。
a programme = 节目单
a theatre programme = 戏院的节目单
⑤ What is the programme for tomorrow morning?
= 明天上午怎么安排？
the programme for tomorrow = 明天的安排
口语：问「今天有什么安排」就说 What's the programme for today?
⑥ Use the short wash programme for clothes that are only slightly dirty.
= 只是稍微有点脏的衣服，用短洗涤程序。
a wash programme = 洗涤程序
指洗衣机、洗碗机设好的一套步骤。
⑦ The final section of the motorway is programmed for completion next year.
= 那条高速公路的最后一段定于明年完工。
be programmed for something = 被安排在某时
这样用多是被动。
⑧ I have programmed the heating to come on at six in the morning.
= 我把暖气设定在早上六点自动打开。
programme something to do something = 设定某物做某事
人「天生就会」也说 be programmed to do something。
⑨ He wrote a small program that sorts the photographs by date.
= 他写了个小程序，把照片按日期排序。
a program = 电脑程序、（美式）节目、计划
write a program = 写程序
a computer program = 电脑程序
⑩ She learned to program at school on a machine that filled a room.
= 她是上学时在一台占满一间屋子的机器上学会编程的。
program = 编程
program a computer = 给电脑编程
⑪ The firm employs forty programmers in an office above a bank.
= 那家公司在一家银行楼上的办公室里雇着四十名程序员。
a programmer = 程序员
a computer programmer = 电脑程序员
⑫ The course teaches programming to children from the age of eight.
= 那门课教八岁以上的孩子编程。
programming = 编程
a programming language = 编程语言
⑬ The station has changed its programming to attract younger listeners.
= 那家电台调整了节目编排，想吸引年轻些的听众。
programming = 节目编排
children's programming = 儿童节目
⑭ The oven has a programmable timer that can be set a week ahead.
= 那台烤箱有个可编程的定时器，可以提前一个星期设定。
programmable = 可编程的
a programmable timer = 可编程的定时器
⑮ The speech was a programmatic statement of what the party would do in office.
= 那篇讲话是那个党上台后要做什么的纲领性声明。
programmatic = 纲领性的
a programmatic statement = 纲领性声明
⑯ The records can be searched programmatically without opening each file.
= 那些记录可以用程序检索，不必一个个打开文件。
programmatically = 通过程序地
⑰ Programme music tells a story or paints a scene, like a storm at sea.
= 标题音乐讲一个故事或描绘一个场面，比如海上的风暴。
programme music = 标题音乐
跟不描绘什么的「纯音乐」相对。

progress
/ˈprəʊɡres/（名）/prəˈɡres/（动）
核心：名词，不可数，重音在前：进步、进展：make progress；前进、行进。习语 in progress 是（正式）正在进行中。
动词，重音在后：进步、进展；（正式）前进、行进；（时间）推移：as the evening progressed。
① She has made great progress in her reading this term.
= 这学期她的阅读进步很大。
make progress = 取得进步
make great progress = 进步很大
② We are making slow progress with the repairs to the roof.
= 我们修屋顶进展很慢。
slow progress = 进展缓慢
progress with something = 某事的进展
③ The ship made slow progress through the ice.
= 那艘船在冰里缓缓前行。
progress through something = 在某处行进
这是较正式的用法。
④ Please do not enter while the examination is in progress.
= 考试进行时请勿入内。
in progress = 正在进行中
work in progress = 还在做的活儿、半成品
⑤ The work is progressing well and should be finished by June.
= 工程进展顺利，六月前应该能完工。
progress well = 进展顺利
动词重音在第二个音节。
⑥ The pupils progress from simple sums to algebra in three years.
= 学生们三年里从简单的加减法学到代数。
progress from A to B = 从甲进到乙
progress to something = 进到某一步
⑦ The crowd grew noisier as the evening progressed.
= 夜越深，人群越吵。
as the evening progressed = 随着夜越来越深
as the day progresses = 随着这一天过下去
名词不可数：不说 a progress。

progression
/prəˈɡreʃn/
核心：三支。一是（一步步的）发展、进展：progression from A to B，不可数或单数；二是连续的一串、系列：a progression of，可数；
三是（数学）级数、数列，可数。
① The drug slows the progression of the disease but does not cure it.
= 那种药延缓病情的发展，却治不好病。
the progression of a disease = 病情的发展
slow the progression = 延缓发展
② There is a natural progression from teaching to running a school.
= 从教书到管一所学校，是顺理成章的一步。
a progression from A to B = 从甲到乙的进展
a natural progression = 顺理成章的一步
③ The firm went through a rapid progression of managers in five years.
= 那家公司五年里走马灯似的换了一串经理。
a progression of managers = 一串接连的经理
a progression of something = 一连串的……
④ The numbers two, four, eight and sixteen form a geometric progression.
= 二、四、八、十六这几个数构成等比数列。
a geometric progression = 等比数列
an arithmetic progression = 等差数列

progressive
/prəˈɡresɪv/
核心：形容词：进步的、主张改革的、开明的：赞成新思想、新做法；逐步的、渐进的、一步步加重的；（税）累进的；（语法）进行时的：the present progressive。
名词：进步人士、改革派，可数。副词 progressively 是逐步地、越来越。政治上的 progressivism 是进步主义，不可数。并入本条。
① The school is known for its progressive methods of teaching.
= 那所学校以开明的教学方法出名。
progressive methods = 开明的方法
progressive ideas = 进步的思想
② There has been a progressive decline in the number of small farms.
= 小农场的数目在逐步减少。
a progressive decline = 逐步的减少
病「进行性的」也用这个词：a progressive disease。
③ A progressive tax takes a larger share from those who earn more.
= 累进税从挣得多的人那里收的比例更高。
a progressive tax = 累进税
反过来是 regressive tax（累退税）。
④ "She is reading" is an example of the present progressive.
= 「She is reading」是现在进行时的例子。
the present progressive = 现在进行时
也叫 the present continuous。
⑤ The party was founded by a group of young progressives in the nineteen twenties.
= 那个党是一九二〇年代一群年轻的改革派创建的。
a progressive = 进步人士、改革派
多用复数。
⑥ His eyesight grew progressively worse over the next ten years.
= 此后十年里，他的视力越来越差。
progressively worse = 越来越差
后面多跟比较级。
⑦ The book traces the rise of progressivism in American politics.
= 那本书梳理了进步主义在美国政治里的兴起。
progressivism = 进步主义
⑧ Her parents sent her to a school that believed in progressive education.
= 她父母把她送进了一所信奉进步教育的学校。
progressive education = 进步教育
指少管束、让孩子自己去发现的那一路教法。

prohibit
/prəˈhɪbɪt/
核心：（正式）两支，及物，多用被动。一是（用法律、规定）禁止：prohibit something、prohibit somebody from doing；二是使不可能、妨碍。
分词 prohibited 作形容词：被禁止的。并入本条。
① Smoking is strictly prohibited in all parts of the hospital.
= 医院各处严禁吸烟。
be strictly prohibited = 严禁
prohibit something = 禁止某事
② The law prohibits shops from selling alcohol to anyone under eighteen.
= 法律禁止商店向未满十八岁的人卖酒。
prohibit somebody from doing something = 禁止某人做某事
介词用 from，不说 prohibit somebody to do。
③ The high cost of the equipment prohibits its use in small clinics.
= 那种设备太贵，小诊所用不起。
prohibit the use of something = 使某物没法用
这一支主语是事，不是人。
④ The officers found several prohibited items in his luggage.
= 官员在他的行李里查出了几件违禁物品。
prohibited items = 违禁物品
a prohibited area = 禁区
比 forbid 正式，多见于法律、告示。

prohibition
/ˌprəʊɪˈbɪʃn/
核心：三支。一是禁止（这件事），不可数：the prohibition of；二是禁令，可数：a prohibition on、against；
三是 Prohibition：（美国一九二〇到一九三三年的）禁酒时期，不可数。主张禁酒的人叫 prohibitionist。并入本条。
① The treaty calls for the prohibition of all chemical weapons.
= 那项条约要求禁止一切化学武器。
the prohibition of something = 对某物的禁止
这一支不可数。
② There is a prohibition on fishing in the lake between March and June.
= 三月到六月之间，那个湖禁止钓鱼。
a prohibition on something = 对某事的禁令
a prohibition against something = 对某事的禁令
③ Her grandfather made a fortune selling whisky during Prohibition.
= 她爷爷在禁酒时期卖威士忌发了财。
Prohibition = （美国的）禁酒时期
首字母大写，不带冠词。
④ The prohibitionists marched through the town with banners against drink.
= 主张禁酒的人举着反对喝酒的横幅在城里游行。
a prohibitionist = 主张禁酒的人

prohibitive
/prəˈhɪbətɪv/
核心：两支。一是（价钱、费用）高得让人买不起、用不起的；二是禁止的、禁止性的。副词 prohibitively，并入本条。
① The cost of flying the whole team to the final is prohibitive.
= 让全队飞去打决赛，费用高得负担不起。
be prohibitive = 高得负担不起
prohibitive costs = 高得负担不起的费用
② The government has put a prohibitive tax on imported cars.
= 政府对进口汽车征收了高得让人不敢买的税。
a prohibitive tax = 高得让人不敢买的税
目的就是让人别买。
③ Houses in the centre of the city are prohibitively expensive.
= 市中心的房子贵得让人不敢问津。
prohibitively expensive = 贵得让人不敢问津
这个副词差不多只跟 expensive、high、costly 搭。

project
/ˈprɒdʒekt/（名）/prəˈdʒekt/（动）
核心：名词，重音在前，可数：（有计划的）工程、项目、方案：a building project；（学生的）课题、专题作业；（美式）the projects 是政府建的廉租住宅区，也说 housing project。
动词，重音在后：预计、推算，多用被动：be projected to；计划、打算，多用被动；把（光、影像）投射到：project something on、onto；
伸出、凸出：project from、over；展现（某种形象）、表现（自己）；让（声音）传得远；
（心理学）project something onto somebody 是把（自己的感受、毛病）投射到别人身上。
① The council has approved a project to build a new bridge over the river.
= 镇议会批准了在河上新建一座桥的工程。
a project to do something = 做某事的工程
a building project = 建筑工程
a project manager = 项目经理
② The children are doing a project on the history of their village.
= 孩子们在做一个关于本村历史的专题作业。
a project on something = 关于某事的专题作业
do a project = 做专题作业
③ He grew up in the projects on the south side of the city.
= 他在城南的廉租住宅区长大。
the projects = 廉租住宅区
a housing project = 廉租住宅区
④ The population of the city is projected to double by the end of the century.
= 预计到本世纪末，那座城市的人口将翻一番。
be projected to do something = 预计会做某事
projected sales = 预计的销量
⑤ The next phase of the work is projected for the spring.
= 工程的下一期定在春天。
be projected for the spring = 定在春天
⑥ The film was projected on to a sheet hung between two trees.
= 影片投映在挂在两棵树之间的一块床单上。
project something on to something = 把某物投射到某物上
on to 也连写作 onto。
⑦ A balcony projects from the front of the house above the door.
= 房子正面的门上方伸出一个阳台。
project from something = 从某处伸出
project over something = 伸到某物上方
⑧ She projects an image of calm, whatever she may be feeling.
= 不管心里怎么想，她给人的印象总是镇定。
project an image = 展现一种形象
project yourself = 表现自己
⑨ Actors are trained to project their voices to the back of the theatre.
= 演员受过训练，能让声音传到剧场的最后一排。
project your voice = 让声音传得远
不是喊，是让声音送得出去。
⑩ He projects his own fears onto his children and will not let them swim.
= 他把自己的恐惧投射到孩子身上，不许他们游泳。
project onto = 投射到……身上
project something onto somebody = 把某种感受投射到某人身上

projectile
/prəˈdʒektaɪl/
核心：（正式，术语）抛射物、弹丸：扔出去、射出去的东西，尤指当武器用的。可数。
① The crowd threw stones, bottles and other projectiles at the police.
= 人群朝警察扔石头、瓶子和别的东西。
a projectile = 抛射物
比 missile 更书面。
② The gun fires a projectile weighing forty kilograms over twenty miles.
= 那门炮能把四十公斤重的炮弹打出二十多英里。
fire a projectile = 发射弹丸
吐得又急又远的呕吐叫 projectile vomiting。

projection
/prəˈdʒekʃn/
核心：名词：（根据现有情况作的）预测、推算，可数；（影像的）投射、放映，不可数；凸出的部分，可数；
（地图的）投影画法，可数；（心理学）投射，不可数。放映员叫 projectionist。并入本条。
① The firm's sales projections for next year turned out to be far too hopeful.
= 那家公司对明年销量的预测后来证明乐观得过头了。
sales projections = 销量预测
make a projection = 作预测
② The lecture was spoiled by the poor projection of the slides.
= 幻灯片放得不清楚，把那场讲座糟蹋了。
the projection of slides = 幻灯片的放映
③ The climber held on to a small projection of rock above his head.
= 登山者抓住了头顶上一小块凸出的岩石。
a projection of rock = 凸出的岩石
a projection = 凸出的部分
④ The projectionist fell asleep, and the screen went white.
= 放映员睡着了，银幕变成一片白。
a projectionist = 放映员

projector
/prəˈdʒektə(r)/
核心：投影仪、放映机：把影片、幻灯片、电脑画面投到幕布上的机器。可数。
① The teacher connected her laptop to the projector on the ceiling.
= 老师把笔记本电脑接到天花板上的投影仪上。
a projector = 投影仪
a slide projector = 幻灯机
② The old film projector rattled so loudly that we could hardly hear the sound.
= 那台旧电影放映机哗啦哗啦响得我们几乎听不见声音。
a film projector = 电影放映机
美式多说 movie projector。

prolapse
/ˈprəʊlæps/
核心：（医学）名词：脱垂：体内的器官从正常的位置滑下来，可数。动词：（器官）脱垂。
① She had an operation to repair a prolapse of the womb.
= 她做了一次修复子宫脱垂的手术。
a prolapse of the womb = 子宫脱垂
a prolapse = 脱垂
② The scan showed that one of the discs in his spine had prolapsed.
= 扫描显示他脊椎里的一块椎间盘突出了。
a disc prolapses = 椎间盘突出
a prolapsed disc = 突出的椎间盘，口语叫 slipped disc。

prole
/prəʊl/
核心：（旧式口语，冒犯）下层人、无产者：proletarian 的截短，带看不起的口气。可数。
① In the novel the proles are the only people whom the state does not bother to watch.
= 那部小说里，无产者是国家唯一懒得监视的人。
the proles = 无产者
因小说《一九八四》而广为人知。
② He complained that the directors treated the staff like proles.
= 他抱怨董事们把员工当下等人对待。
treat somebody like a prole = 把某人当下等人
说人是 prole 很失礼。

proletarian
/ˌprəʊləˈteəriən/
核心：形容词：无产阶级的、工人阶级的。名词：无产者，可数。这个阶级总称 the proletariat，单数，动词单复数都行；多见于政治、历史论述。
① The poet came from a proletarian family in a mining town.
= 那位诗人出身矿区小镇的工人家庭。
a proletarian family = 工人家庭
proletarian origins = 工人出身
② The pamphlet called on the proletarians of every country to unite.
= 那本小册子号召各国无产者联合起来。
a proletarian = 无产者
日常说 working-class person。
③ The factories drew the rural poor into the cities and created an urban proletariat.
= 工厂把乡下的穷人吸引进城，造就了城市无产阶级。
the proletariat = 无产阶级
对着说的是 the bourgeoisie（资产阶级）。

proliferate
/prəˈlɪfəreɪt/
核心：激增、迅速繁殖、扩散：数量很快地增多。不及物。名词 proliferation，不可数或单数。并入本条。
① Coffee shops have proliferated in the town centre in the last five years.
= 近五年镇中心的咖啡店猛增。
proliferate = 激增
主语是越来越多的那样东西。
② The cells proliferate rapidly when the tissue is damaged.
= 组织受损时，那些细胞迅速增殖。
proliferate rapidly = 迅速增殖
cells proliferate = 细胞增殖
③ The treaty is meant to stop the proliferation of nuclear weapons.
= 那项条约旨在阻止核武器扩散。
the proliferation of something = 某物的扩散、激增
nuclear proliferation = 核扩散

prolific
/prəˈlɪfɪk/
核心：三支。一是（作家、艺术家）多产的；二是（植物、动物）结实多的、繁殖力强的；三是（球员）进球多的、得分多的。副词 prolifically，并入本条。
① She is a prolific writer who has published forty novels in thirty years.
= 她是位多产作家，三十年出了四十部小说。
a prolific writer = 多产作家
a prolific author = 多产作者
② Rabbits are prolific breeders and can produce six litters a year.
= 兔子繁殖力很强，一年能生六窝。
a prolific breeder = 繁殖力强的动物
果树结得多也说 a prolific tree。
③ He was the most prolific goalscorer in the history of the club.
= 他是那家俱乐部历史上进球最多的射手。
a prolific goalscorer = 进球多的射手
a prolific scorer = 得分多的球员
④ The plant flowers prolifically from June until the first frost.
= 那种植物从六月到头一场霜一直开得很盛。
prolifically = 大量地
write prolifically = 写得多

prolix
/ˈprəʊlɪks/
核心：（正式）（文字、讲话）冗长的、啰唆的。名词 prolixity，不可数。并入本条。
① The report is so prolix that few people read beyond the first chapter.
= 那份报告太冗长，没几个人读得过第一章。
be prolix = 冗长
是批评的话：长而无当。
② He is a prolix speaker who never uses one word where ten will do.
= 他说话啰唆，能用十个词的地方绝不只用一个。
a prolix speaker = 说话啰唆的人
prolix prose = 冗长的文字
③ The editor cut a third of the book to cure its prolixity.
= 编辑删掉了那本书的三分之一，治它的冗长。
prolixity = 冗长

prologue
/ˈprəʊlɒɡ/
核心：两支，可数。一是（戏剧、诗、书的）序幕、开场白、序诗；二是 a prologue to：（一连串事件的）序幕、开端。美式也拼作 prolog。
① The play begins with a prologue spoken by an actor alone on the stage.
= 那出戏以一位演员独自在台上念的开场白开头。
a prologue = 开场白
the prologue to a play = 一出戏的序幕
② The small strike was a prologue to a year of unrest in the factories.
= 那次小罢工是工厂一年动荡的序幕。
a prologue to something = 某事的序幕
这是比喻的用法，多带单数。
对应的结尾部分叫 epilogue。

prolong
/prəˈlɒŋ/
核心：延长、拖长：让一件事持续得更久。及物。分词 prolonged 作形容词：长时间的、持续很久的；名词 prolongation。并入本条。
① The operation could prolong her life by several years.
= 那个手术能让她多活几年。
prolong somebody's life = 延长某人的生命
prolong something = 延长某事
② I do not want to prolong the meeting, so I shall be brief.
= 我不想把会拖长，就长话短说。
prolong a meeting = 把会拖长
延长期限、签证不用这个词，用 extend。
③ The town was cut off after a prolonged period of heavy rain.
= 大雨下了很长一段时间之后，那座城与外界隔绝了。
a prolonged period = 很长一段时间
prolonged applause = 经久不息的掌声
④ Neither side wants a prolongation of the strike into the winter.
= 双方都不想让罢工拖到冬天。
a prolongation of something = 某事的延长
the prolongation of life = 生命的延长
习语 prolong the agony 是把难熬的事拖得更久。

prom
/prɒm/
核心：三支，可数。一是（美式）（中学、大学学年末的）正式舞会；二是（英式口语）海滨步道：promenade 的截短；
三是（英式）站席音乐会：promenade concert 的截短，the Proms 特指伦敦每年夏天办的那一季。
① She spent weeks choosing a dress to wear to the school prom.
= 她花了好几个星期挑一条去学校舞会穿的裙子。
the school prom = 学校的舞会
the senior prom = 毕业班舞会
② We walked along the prom eating chips out of the paper.
= 我们沿着海滨步道边走边吃纸包着的薯条。
along the prom = 沿着海滨步道
这一支只在英式口语里。
③ They queued all afternoon for tickets to the last night of the Proms.
= 为了买逍遥音乐节最后一夜的票，他们排了一下午的队。
the Proms = 逍遥音乐节
a prom = 站席音乐会

promenade
/ˌprɒməˈnɑːd/
核心：名词，可数：（英式）海滨步道：沿海修的供人散步的宽路；（旧式）（在公共场所的）散步、漫步。动词：（旧式）（在公共场所）散步、漫步。
英式 promenade concert 是站席音乐会：一部分听众站着听的古典音乐会。并入本条。
① The hotel stands on the promenade, facing the pier and the sea.
= 那家旅馆在海滨步道上，对着栈桥和大海。
on the promenade = 在海滨步道上
a promenade = 海滨步道
② The families of the town took their evening promenade in the square.
= 镇上的人家傍晚到广场上散步。
an evening promenade = 傍晚的散步
这一支如今显得老派。
③ Couples promenaded along the sea front in their best clothes.
= 一对对男女穿着最好的衣服在海边漫步。
promenade along something = 沿着某处漫步
走给人看的那种散步。
④ The orchestra gives a series of promenade concerts in the hall every summer.
= 那支乐团每年夏天在那座大厅办一系列站席音乐会。
a promenade concert = 站席音乐会
promenade concerts = 站席音乐会

Promethean
/prəˈmiːθiən/
核心：（文学）普罗米修斯式的：像希腊神话里盗火给人的普罗米修斯那样，敢于反抗、有创造力、不惜受苦的。首字母大写。
① The poet saw the scientist as a Promethean figure who defied the gods.
= 那位诗人把科学家看成一个违抗众神的普罗米修斯式人物。
a Promethean figure = 普罗米修斯式的人物
多放在名词前。
② Building the canal through the mountains was a Promethean task.
= 开那条穿山的运河是一项艰巨无比的壮举。
a Promethean task = 艰巨无比的壮举
也指胆子大、规模大得惊人的。

prominent
/ˈprɒmɪnənt/
核心：三支。一是重要的、著名的、杰出的；二是显眼的、突出的：一眼就看得见；三是凸出的、高起的。
副词 prominently；名词 prominence 是重要、显著、出名，不可数或单数。并入本条。
① She is a prominent lawyer who has argued several famous cases.
= 她是位著名律师，办过好几桩出名的案子。
a prominent lawyer = 著名律师
play a prominent part = 起重要作用
② The notice was put up in a prominent position by the main door.
= 那张通知贴在正门旁边一个显眼的位置。
a prominent position = 显眼的位置
a prominent place = 显眼的地方
③ He has a thin face with a prominent nose and chin.
= 他脸很瘦，鼻子和下巴都很突出。
a prominent nose = 高鼻子
prominent cheekbones = 高颧骨
④ Her photograph was prominently displayed on the wall behind the desk.
= 她的照片醒目地挂在书桌后面的墙上。
be prominently displayed = 醒目地陈列着
feature prominently = 占突出的地位
⑤ The young singer came to prominence after winning a television contest.
= 那位年轻歌手赢了一场电视比赛之后出了名。
come to prominence = 出名
give prominence to something = 突出某事

promiscuous
/prəˈmɪskjuəs/
核心：两支。一是（含贬义）性关系随便的、滥交的；二是（正式）混杂的、不加选择的。
副词 promiscuously；名词 promiscuity，不可数。并入本条。
① The article claimed that young people were more promiscuous than their parents had been.
= 那篇文章声称年轻人比父辈当年更随便。
be promiscuous = 性关系随便
promiscuous behaviour = 滥交的行为
② The shop was a promiscuous jumble of books, clocks and old furniture.
= 那家店里书、钟和旧家具乱七八糟堆在一起。
a promiscuous jumble = 乱七八糟的一堆
这一支是正式用法，少见。
③ The campaign warned teenagers about the risks of sexual promiscuity.
= 那场宣传提醒青少年当心滥交的风险。
sexual promiscuity = 滥交
promiscuity = 性关系随便
④ He quotes promiscuously from poets, pop songs and old advertisements.
= 他引用起来不分青红皂白，诗人、流行歌、老广告都引。
promiscuously = 不加选择地

promise
/ˈprɒmɪs/
核心：动词：答应、许诺、保证：promise to do、promise (somebody) that、promise somebody something；使很可能、预示：promise to be。
名词：诺言、许诺，可数：make、keep、break a promise；（会有出息、会成功的）迹象、希望，不可数：show promise。
习语：promise the moon、promise the earth 是开空头支票、许下办不到的诺；I can't promise anything 是我不敢打包票；
I promise you 是（口语）我跟你保证，用来加强语气；the Promised Land 是（圣经里上帝许给以色列人的）应许之地，也比喻盼望中的好地方。
分词 promising 作形容词：有希望的、有前途的，副词 promisingly。并入本条。
① She promised to write to her mother every week from the camp.
= 她答应每个星期从营地给母亲写信。
promise to do something = 答应做某事
② He promised me that he would never mention the matter again.
= 他向我保证再也不提这件事。
promise somebody that… = 向某人保证……
promise that… = 保证……
③ I have promised the children a trip to the zoo on Saturday.
= 我答应了孩子们星期六带他们去动物园。
promise somebody something = 答应给某人某物
promise something to somebody = 把某物许给某人
④ "I will be back before dark, I promise," she called from the gate.
= 「我天黑前一定回来，我保证。」她在院门口喊道。
I promise = 我保证
放在句末、句中都行。
⑤ It promises to be an exciting match, with both sides at full strength.
= 双方都是全部主力上场，看来会是一场精彩的比赛。
promise to be something = 看来会是……
主语是事，不是人。
⑥ The dark clouds in the west promised rain before the evening.
= 西边的乌云预示着傍晚前有雨。
promise rain = 预示有雨
promise something = 预示某事
⑦ He made a promise to his father that he would finish his studies.
= 他向父亲许下诺言，一定把书念完。
make a promise = 许下诺言
a promise to do something = 做某事的诺言
⑧ She kept her promise and came back for the dog a year later.
= 她说话算数，一年后回来接那条狗。
keep a promise = 守信、说话算数
keep your promise = 守信
⑨ He broke his promise and told the newspapers everything.
= 他食言了，把什么都告诉了报纸。
break a promise = 食言
break your promise = 食言
⑩ The boy shows great promise as a pianist and practises four hours a day.
= 那个男孩很有钢琴家的苗子，一天练四个小时。
show promise = 有出息的苗头
a young player of promise = 有前途的年轻球员
⑪ The spring sunshine brought the promise of a good harvest.
= 春天的阳光带来了丰收的希望。
the promise of something = 某事的希望
这一支不可数。
⑫ The candidates promised the moon and delivered nothing once they were elected.
= 候选人许得天花乱坠，当选之后什么也没兑现。
promise the moon = 开空头支票
promise the earth = 许下办不到的诺
⑬ I will try to get you a ticket, but I can't promise anything.
= 我尽量给你弄张票，但不敢打包票。
I can't promise anything = 我不敢打包票
话说在前头的说法。
⑭ You will not regret it, I promise you, if you take the job.
= 我跟你保证，接下这份工作你不会后悔的。
I promise you = 我跟你保证
是加强语气，不是真的在许诺。
⑮ To the people on the crowded ships, America was the Promised Land.
= 对挤在船上的那些人来说，美国就是应许之地。
the Promised Land = 应许之地
首字母大写。
⑯ She is one of the most promising young writers in the country.
= 她是国内最有前途的年轻作家之一。
promising = 有前途的
a promising start = 良好的开端
⑰ The season began promisingly, with three wins in the first four games.
= 那个赛季开局不错，头四场赢了三场。
promisingly = 有希望地
begin promisingly = 开局不错

promo
/ˈprəʊməʊ/
核心：（口语）宣传片、宣传活动、促销品：promotion 的截短。可数，复数 promos。也作定语：a promo video。
① The band shot a promo for the new single on a beach in Wales.
= 那支乐队在威尔士的一片海滩上给新单曲拍了宣传片。
a promo = 宣传片
shoot a promo = 拍宣传片
② The shop is giving away promo copies of the magazine this week.
= 那家店这个星期在送那本杂志的赠阅本。
a promo copy = 赠阅本
a promo video = 宣传视频

promontory
/ˈprɒməntri/
核心：岬、海角：伸进海里或湖里的一块高而窄的陆地。可数，复数 promontories。
① The lighthouse stands on a rocky promontory at the mouth of the bay.
= 灯塔立在海湾口一块多石的岬角上。
a rocky promontory = 多石的岬角
a promontory = 岬、海角
② From the promontory we could see the whole length of the coast.
= 从那个海角上，我们看得见整条海岸线。
from the promontory = 从海角上
比 headland 书面一些。

promote
/prəˈməʊt/
核心：及物。一是促进、推动、增进：promote growth；二是提升、晋升，多用被动：be promoted to，反义 demote；
三是促销、宣传、推广（产品、活动）；四是（体育）让（球队）升级，多用被动，反义 relegate。
① The organization works to promote understanding between the two countries.
= 那个组织致力于增进两国之间的了解。
promote understanding = 增进了解
promote growth = 促进增长
② She was promoted to head of department after only three years.
= 她只干了三年就升了系主任。
be promoted to something = 升为某职
promote somebody = 提升某人
③ He was promoted from sergeant to captain during the war.
= 战争期间他从中士升到了上尉。
be promoted from A to B = 从甲升到乙
职位前不加冠词。
④ The singer is touring the country to promote her new album.
= 那位歌手正在全国巡回，宣传她的新专辑。
promote an album = 宣传专辑
promote a product = 推销产品
⑤ The firm promotes the drink as a healthy alternative to coffee.
= 那家公司把这种饮料当作代替咖啡的健康之选来推销。
promote something as something = 把某物当作某物来推销
⑥ The club was promoted to the first division at the end of the season.
= 赛季结束时，那家俱乐部升入了甲级。
be promoted to the first division = 升入甲级
这一支是英式足球的说法。

promoter
/prəˈməʊtə(r)/
核心：两支，可数。一是（演出、比赛的）主办人、承办人；二是 a promoter of：（一种主张、一项事业的）倡导者、推动者。
① The promoter cancelled the concert when only two hundred tickets were sold.
= 只卖出两百张票，主办人把音乐会取消了。
a promoter = 主办人
a boxing promoter = 拳击比赛的承办人
② She has been a tireless promoter of women's sport for thirty years.
= 三十年来她一直不知疲倦地推动女子体育。
a promoter of something = 某事的倡导者
a tireless promoter = 不知疲倦的推动者

promotion
/prəˈməʊʃn/
核心：四支。一是提升、晋升，可数也可不可数；二是促销、宣传、推广活动，可数也可不可数；
三是（正式）促进、提倡，不可数：the promotion of；四是（球队的）升级，不可数。
形容词 promotional 是促销的、宣传的。并入本条。
① She got a promotion after only a year and moved to the London office.
= 她才干了一年就升了职，调到了伦敦办事处。
get a promotion = 升职
promotion to manager = 升任经理
② There is little chance of promotion in a firm as small as this.
= 在这么小的公司里，升职的机会很少。
a chance of promotion = 升职的机会
这样用不可数。
③ The supermarket is running a special promotion on French cheese this month.
= 那家超市这个月在搞法国奶酪的特别促销。
a special promotion = 特别促销
a sales promotion = 促销活动
④ The society was founded for the promotion of the Welsh language.
= 那个学会是为了推广威尔士语而成立的。
the promotion of something = 对某事的促进、提倡
这一支是正式用法。
⑤ The team won promotion to the top division for the first time.
= 那支球队头一回升入了顶级联赛。
win promotion = 升级
反过来是 relegation（降级）。
⑥ The author spent a month on a promotional tour of twenty cities.
= 那位作家花了一个月巡回二十座城市作宣传。
a promotional tour = 巡回宣传
promotional material = 宣传材料

prompt
/prɒmpt/
核心：形容词：迅速的、立刻的、不耽搁的；（人）准时的，不放在名词前。
动词：促使、引起：prompt somebody to do；（用提问、提醒）鼓励……往下说；给（演员）提词。
名词，可数：（给演员的）提词；（电脑屏幕上的）提示符、提示。副词：（英式口语）准时地、整：at six o'clock prompt。
派生：promptly 是迅速地、准时地、立刻；promptness 是迅速；prompter 是提词员；prompting 是催促、劝说，可数也可不可数。并入本条。
① Thank you for your prompt reply to my letter of last week.
= 谢谢您及时回复我上星期的信。
a prompt reply = 及时的回复
prompt action = 迅速的行动
② Please be prompt, because the coach will not wait for anyone.
= 请准时，大巴不等任何人。
be prompt = 准时
be prompt in doing something = 做某事不拖延
③ The accident prompted the council to put a crossing outside the school.
= 那起事故促使镇议会在学校外面设了人行横道。
prompt somebody to do something = 促使某人做某事
④ The minister's remarks prompted an angry response from the unions.
= 部长的话引起了工会愤怒的反应。
prompt a response = 引起反应
prompt something = 引起某事
⑤ "And then what happened?" he prompted, when she fell silent.
= 她不作声了，他提了一句：「后来呢？」
prompt = 提醒人往下说
prompt somebody = 提示某人
⑥ She forgot her lines twice and had to be prompted from the wings.
= 她忘了两次词，只好由人在侧幕提词。
be prompted = 由人提词
prompt an actor = 给演员提词
⑦ The actor dried up completely and needed a prompt.
= 那位演员彻底忘了词，得有人提一下。
a prompt = 提词
give somebody a prompt = 给某人提词
⑧ Type your password at the prompt and press the return key.
= 在提示符处输入密码，再按回车键。
at the prompt = 在提示符处
a prompt = 提示符、提示
⑨ The meeting will begin at ten o'clock prompt.
= 会议十点整开始。
at ten o'clock prompt = 十点整
这样用是英式口语，放在钟点后面。
⑩ She arrived promptly at nine and left on the stroke of five.
= 她九点准时到，五点一到就走。
promptly at nine = 九点准时
arrive promptly = 准时到
⑪ He sat down in the armchair and promptly fell asleep.
= 他在扶手椅上一坐下，立刻就睡着了。
promptly = 立刻、马上
多指紧接着前一件事。
⑫ The staff were praised for the promptness with which they dealt with the fire.
= 员工处置火情迅速，受到了表扬。
promptness = 迅速
with promptness = 迅速地
⑬ The prompter sat hidden in a box at the front of the stage.
= 提词员坐在舞台前沿的一个小间里，观众看不见。
a prompter = 提词员
⑭ He tidied his room without any prompting from his parents.
= 没等父母催，他自己把房间收拾了。
without prompting = 不用人催
at somebody's prompting = 在某人的催促下

promulgate
/ˈprɒmlɡeɪt/
核心：（正式）两支，及物，多用被动。一是传播、宣扬（一种思想、信仰）；二是颁布、公布（新法律、新制度）。
名词 promulgation，不可数。并入本条。
① The new doctrine was promulgated by missionaries throughout the islands.
= 传教士把那套新教义传遍了各个岛。
promulgate a doctrine = 传播教义
promulgate an idea = 宣扬一种主张
② The new constitution was promulgated in January of the following year.
= 新宪法是第二年一月颁布的。
promulgate a constitution = 颁布宪法
promulgate a law = 颁布法律
③ The law comes into force thirty days after its promulgation.
= 那项法律颁布三十天后生效。
promulgation = 颁布
the promulgation of a law = 法律的颁布

prone
/prəʊn/
核心：两支。一是 prone to、prone to do：容易（遭受不好的事）的、有……倾向的，也接在名词后构成复合词：accident-prone；
二是（正式）俯卧的、趴着的，反义 supine。名词 proneness，不可数。并入本条。
① Old houses near the river are prone to damp in the winter.
= 河边的老房子冬天容易返潮。
be prone to something = 容易遭受某事
prone to injury = 容易受伤
② He is prone to lose his temper when he is tired.
= 他一累就容易发脾气。
be prone to do something = 容易做某事
跟的都是不好的事。
③ My brother is so accident-prone that he breaks a bone almost every year.
= 我弟弟特别容易出事故，差不多每年都要摔断一根骨头。
accident-prone = 容易出事故的
injury-prone = 容易受伤的
④ The victim was found lying prone on the kitchen floor.
= 人们发现受害者脸朝下趴在厨房的地上。
lie prone = 俯卧
in a prone position = 呈俯卧姿势
⑤ The doctor asked about any proneness to allergies in the family.
= 医生问家里有没有人容易过敏。
proneness to something = 容易得某病、遭某事的倾向

prong
/prɒŋ/
核心：两支，可数。一是（叉子、耙子的）尖齿、叉齿；二是（一项计划、一场进攻的）几个方面之一、几路之一。
-pronged 构成形容词：a three-pronged attack 是三路进攻。
① One of the prongs of the garden fork had snapped off in the hard ground.
= 园艺叉的一根齿在硬地里折断了。
the prongs of a fork = 叉子的齿
a prong = 尖齿
② The first prong of the plan is to cut the cost of fuel.
= 那个计划的头一个方面是降低燃料的开支。
a prong of a plan = 计划的一个方面
这是比喻的用法。
③ The general launched a three-pronged attack on the city at dawn.
= 拂晓时，将军兵分三路向那座城发起进攻。
a three-pronged attack = 三路进攻
a two-pronged approach = 双管齐下的办法

pronoun
/ˈprəʊnaʊn/
核心：（语法）代词：代替名词或名词短语的词，如 she、it、this、who。可数。形容词 pronominal 是代词的、作代词用的。并入本条。
① In the sentence "She gave it to him" there are three pronouns.
= 「She gave it to him」这个句子里有三个代词。
a pronoun = 代词
a personal pronoun = 人称代词
② The form asks which pronouns you would like people to use for you.
= 那张表问你希望别人用哪个代词称呼你。
your pronouns = 称呼你用的代词
这是近年的用法。
③ The chapter deals with pronominal forms such as "mine" and "yours".
= 那一章讲「mine」「yours」这类代词形式。
pronominal = 代词的
a pronominal form = 代词形式

pronounce
/prəˈnaʊns/
核心：及物。一是发（音）、读（一个词）；二是（正式）宣布、宣告、断言：pronounce somebody dead、pronounce sentence。
形容词 pronounceable 是读得出来的，反义 unpronounceable。并入本条。
① How do you pronounce the name of the village where you were born?
= 你出生的那个村子的名字怎么读？
pronounce a name = 读一个名字
pronounce a word = 读一个词
② The "b" in "doubt" is not pronounced.
= 「doubt」里的「b」不发音。
be pronounced = 发音
not be pronounced = 不发音
③ The driver was pronounced dead on arrival at the hospital.
= 司机送到医院时被宣告死亡。
pronounce somebody dead = 宣告某人死亡
pronounce somebody fit = 宣布某人身体合格
④ The judge will pronounce sentence on the three men tomorrow morning.
= 法官明天上午对那三个人宣判。
pronounce sentence = 宣判
pronounce judgement = 作出裁决
⑤ She tasted the soup and pronounced it excellent.
= 她尝了尝汤，说好极了。
pronounce something excellent = 断言某物极好
口气郑重，也常带点玩笑。
⑥ The firm wanted a name that was short and easily pronounceable in any language.
= 那家公司想要一个短的、哪种语言都好读的名字。
pronounceable = 读得出来的
easily pronounceable = 好读的
⑦ The minister refused to pronounce on the case while it was still before the courts.
= 案子还在法院审着，部长不肯对它发表意见。
pronounce on something = 对某事发表意见、作出判断
pronounce upon something = 对某事发表意见
注意名词拼作 pronunciation，中间没有 o。

pronounced
/prəˈnaʊnst/
核心：明显的、显著的、很容易看出或听出的。多说口音、跛、差别、看法。
① He speaks English with a pronounced Scottish accent.
= 他说英语带着很重的苏格兰口音。
a pronounced accent = 很重的口音
② She walks with a pronounced limp since the accident.
= 那次事故以后，她走路跛得很明显。
a pronounced limp = 明显的跛
③ The difference between the two groups was most pronounced among the youngest children.
= 两组之间的差别在最小的孩子当中最明显。
be pronounced = 明显
become more pronounced = 变得更明显

pronouncement
/prəˈnaʊnsmənt/
核心：（正式）声明、公告、表态：对一件事正式、公开表示的意见或决定。可数。常说 a pronouncement on。
① The Pope made a pronouncement on the subject in his Easter address.
= 教皇在复活节讲话里就那个问题表了态。
make a pronouncement = 发表声明
a pronouncement on something = 关于某事的声明
② Nobody takes his pronouncements about the economy very seriously any more.
= 他对经济发表的那些高论，已经没人太当真了。
his pronouncements = 他的高论
也带讽刺：一本正经的断言。

pronto
/ˈprɒntəʊ/
核心：（口语）马上、赶快、立刻。副词，多放在句末，带催促的口气。
① Get the doctor over here pronto, because he has stopped breathing.
= 赶快把医生叫过来，他没呼吸了。
pronto = 马上、赶快
do something pronto = 马上做某事
② I want those boxes out of the corridor pronto, before the inspector arrives.
= 检查员来之前，马上把过道里那些箱子给我搬走。
want something done pronto = 要某事马上办
是从西班牙语来的口语词。

pronunciation
/prəˌnʌnsiˈeɪʃn/
核心：两支。一是（一个词、一种语言的）发音、读法，可数也可不可数；二是（一个人的）发音：说得准不准，单数或不可数。
拼写里是 -nun-，不是 -noun-；读音也是 /nʌn/。
① The dictionary gives the British and the American pronunciation of every word.
= 那本词典每个词都标出英式和美式的读音。
the pronunciation of a word = 一个词的读音
a guide to pronunciation = 发音指南
② There are two pronunciations of "either", and both are correct.
= 「either」有两种读法，都对。
two pronunciations = 两种读法
这样用可数。
③ Her grammar is good, but her pronunciation needs a lot of work.
= 她语法不错，可是发音还得下大功夫。
her pronunciation = 她的发音
improve your pronunciation = 改进发音

proof
/pruːf/
核心：名词：证据、证明，不可数：proof of、proof that；（数学的）证明、（验算的）过程，可数；（印刷的）校样，可数，多用复数；
（烈酒的）标准酒精度，不可数。形容词：proof against 是（正式）抵挡得住……的；也接在名词后构成复合词：waterproof、bulletproof。
动词：给（布料）做防水处理；也等于 proofread。
习语：the proof of the pudding (is in the eating) 是东西好不好要试了才知道；living proof 是活生生的证明；
proof positive 是确证、铁证。
① The police have no proof that he was in the house that night.
= 警方没有证据证明他那天夜里在那栋房子里。
proof that… = ……的证据
have no proof = 没有证据
② You will need to bring proof of your age, such as a passport.
= 你得带上年龄证明，比如护照。
proof of something = 某事的证明
proof of identity = 身份证明
③ The teacher asked her to write out the proof on the board.
= 老师让她把证明过程写在黑板上。
a proof = （数学的）证明
这一支可数。
④ The author is checking the proofs of her new novel this week.
= 那位作家这个星期在看她新小说的校样。
the proofs = 校样
check the proofs = 看校样
⑤ The rum is seventy per cent proof and should be drunk with care.
= 那种朗姆酒标准酒精度七十，得悠着点喝。
seventy per cent proof = 标准酒精度七十
英美的算法不一样。
⑥ No lock is proof against a thief with enough time and the right tools.
= 贼只要有足够的时间和合适的工具，没有哪把锁挡得住。
be proof against something = 抵挡得住某物
这一支是正式用法。
⑦ The windows of the car are bulletproof and cannot be opened.
= 那辆车的窗玻璃是防弹的，打不开。
bulletproof = 防弹的
childproof = 小孩弄不开的
⑧ The plan looks good on paper, but the proof of the pudding is in the eating.
= 那个计划纸面上看着不错，可行不行得试了才知道。
the proof of the pudding = 好不好试了才知道
全句是 the proof of the pudding is in the eating。
⑨ She is living proof that you can start a new career at sixty.
= 她就是活生生的证明：六十岁照样可以开始新的事业。
living proof = 活生生的证明
be living proof of something = 是某事活生生的证明
⑩ The fingerprints on the glass were proof positive that he had been there.
= 玻璃杯上的指纹是他到过那里的铁证。
proof positive = 确证、铁证
形容词放在后面，是固定说法。

proofread
/ˈpruːfriːd/
核心：校对：把稿子、校样读一遍，找出并改正错字。及物也可不及物；过去式、过去分词 proofread，读 /ˈpruːfred/。
做这件事的人叫 proofreader。并入本条。
① Always proofread your essay before you hand it in to the teacher.
= 作文交给老师之前，一定要校对一遍。
proofread an essay = 校对作文
proofread something = 校对某物
② The letter had clearly not been proofread, for it was full of mistakes.
= 那封信显然没校对过，满篇都是错。
be proofread = 经过校对
过去分词拼法不变，读音变。
③ She works at home as a proofreader for a firm that publishes law books.
= 她在家给一家出法律书的公司做校对。
a proofreader = 校对员
work as a proofreader = 做校对

prop
/prɒp/
核心：名词，可数：支柱、支撑物；比喻靠山、精神支柱；（戏剧、电影的）道具，多用复数；（橄榄球）支柱前锋；（口语）螺旋桨：propeller 的截短。
动词：把……靠在、支在：prop something against、on；用东西把……撑住：prop the door open。
短语动词 prop up 是撑住、支起，也指扶持、支撑（快垮的政权、行业）。过去式 propped。
（口语）props 是应得的称赞、敬意：give somebody props。
① The roof of the old mine was held up by wooden props.
= 那座旧矿井的顶是用木头支柱撑着的。
a wooden prop = 木头支柱
a pit prop = 坑木
② After his wife died, his work became his only prop.
= 妻子死后，工作成了他唯一的精神支柱。
a prop = 靠山、精神支柱
这是比喻的用法。
③ She is in charge of the props for the school play this term.
= 这学期学校那出戏的道具归她管。
the props = 道具
a stage prop = 舞台道具
④ He propped his bicycle against the wall and went into the shop.
= 他把自行车靠在墙上，进了商店。
prop something against something = 把某物靠在某物上
prop something on something = 把某物支在某物上
⑤ She propped the door open with a chair to let the air in.
= 她用一把椅子把门撑开，好让空气进来。
prop the door open = 把门撑开
prop something open = 把某物撑开
⑥ He propped himself up on one elbow and looked at the clock.
= 他用一只胳膊肘撑起身子，看了看钟。
prop yourself up = 撑起身子
prop up = 撑住、支起
⑦ The government spent millions to prop up the failing steel industry.
= 政府花了几百万扶持快垮的钢铁业。
prop up an industry = 扶持一个行业
prop up a regime = 支撑一个政权
⑧ You have to give her props for finishing the race with a broken toe.
= 她断了一根脚趾还跑完了全程，这一点你不能不佩服。
give somebody props = 佩服某人、称赞某人
这是美式口语。

propaganda
/ˌprɒpəˈɡændə/
核心：（多含贬义）宣传、鼓吹：为了让人接受一种政治主张而散布的、常常片面或不实的说法。不可数。
搞宣传的人叫 propagandist，可数；动词 propagandize 是（正式，含贬义）搞宣传，英式也拼作 propagandise。并入本条。
① The film was a piece of enemy propaganda made to frighten the troops.
= 那部片子是敌方的宣传品，拍来吓唬士兵的。
enemy propaganda = 敌方的宣传
a piece of propaganda = 一件宣传品
② The party ran a propaganda campaign in the newspapers before the vote.
= 投票之前，那个党在报上搞了一场宣传攻势。
a propaganda campaign = 宣传攻势
这个词带贬义：片面、不尽不实。
③ He worked as a propagandist for the regime throughout the war.
= 整个战争期间他都在替那个政权搞宣传。
a propagandist = 搞宣传的人
④ The teachers were accused of propagandizing in the classroom.
= 那些教师被指责在课堂上搞宣传。
propagandize = 搞宣传
这个动词少见，带贬义。

propagate
/ˈprɒpəɡeɪt/
核心：三支。一是（正式）传播、宣传（思想、信仰、消息），及物；二是繁殖（植物）、（植物）繁殖，及物也可不及物；
三是（物理）（波、声、光）传播。名词 propagation，不可数；繁殖幼苗用的保温育苗箱叫 propagator。并入本条。
① The group uses the internet to propagate its ideas among the young.
= 那个团体利用互联网在年轻人当中传播自己的主张。
propagate ideas = 传播主张
propagate a belief = 传播一种信仰
② The plant is easy to propagate from cuttings taken in the spring.
= 那种植物用春天剪的插条很容易繁殖。
propagate a plant = 繁殖一种植物
propagate from cuttings = 用插条繁殖
③ Sound waves propagate more quickly through water than through air.
= 声波在水里比在空气里传得快。
waves propagate = 波传播
这是物理学里的说法。
④ The book explains the propagation of roses by grafting.
= 那本书讲怎样用嫁接繁殖玫瑰。
the propagation of something = 某物的繁殖、传播
⑤ She raises her tomato seedlings in a heated propagator on the windowsill.
= 她在窗台上一个带加热的育苗箱里育番茄苗。
a propagator = 育苗箱

propane
/ˈprəʊpeɪn/
核心：丙烷：一种无色的气体，压成液体装在罐子里，用来烧饭、取暖。不可数。
① The stove in the caravan runs on propane from a cylinder outside.
= 房车里的炉子烧的是车外一个罐子里的丙烷。
run on propane = 烧丙烷
propane = 丙烷
② We took a small propane heater to keep the tent warm at night.
= 我们带了一个小丙烷取暖器，夜里给帐篷取暖。
a propane heater = 丙烷取暖器
a propane cylinder = 丙烷罐

propel
/prəˈpel/
核心：两支，及物，多用被动。一是推进、推动：让……往前走；二是驱使、促使、把（某人）推到（某种境地）：propel somebody into、towards。
过去式 propelled。-propelled 构成形容词：jet-propelled。
① The boat is propelled by a small engine at the back.
= 那条船由船尾的一台小发动机推进。
be propelled by something = 由某物推进
propel a boat = 推动船
② He took her by the arm and propelled her gently towards the door.
= 他抓住她的胳膊，轻轻把她往门口推。
propel somebody towards something = 把某人往某处推
③ The success of the film propelled her to fame almost overnight.
= 那部片子的成功让她几乎一夜成名。
propel somebody to fame = 把某人推上成名的路
propel somebody into something = 把某人推进某种境地
④ The army relied on rocket-propelled grenades to stop the tanks.
= 那支军队靠火箭推进的榴弹挡坦克。
rocket-propelled = 火箭推进的
jet-propelled = 喷气推进的

propellant
/prəˈpelənt/
核心：两支，可数也可不可数。一是（喷雾罐里把东西压出来的）喷射剂、推进气体；二是（火箭、枪炮的）推进剂、发射药。
① The spray uses compressed air as a propellant in place of harmful gases.
= 那种喷雾用压缩空气当喷射剂，代替有害的气体。
a propellant = 喷射剂
use something as a propellant = 用某物当喷射剂
② The rocket carries four hundred tonnes of liquid propellant in its tanks.
= 那枚火箭的燃料箱里装着四百吨液体推进剂。
liquid propellant = 液体推进剂
solid propellant = 固体推进剂

propeller
/prəˈpelə(r)/
核心：螺旋桨：装在船尾或飞机上、靠转动的叶片把船或飞机往前推的装置。可数。口语简称 prop。
① The fishing net had wrapped itself round the propeller of the boat.
= 渔网缠在了船的螺旋桨上。
the propeller of a boat = 船的螺旋桨
a propeller = 螺旋桨
② It was a small plane with a single propeller on its nose.
= 那是一架机头只有一个螺旋桨的小飞机。
a single propeller = 单螺旋桨
a propeller blade = 螺旋桨的叶片

propensity
/prəˈpensəti/
核心：（正式）（天生的）倾向、习性：propensity for、propensity to do。可数，复数 propensities。
① He has a propensity for getting into arguments with strangers on trains.
= 他有个毛病，爱在火车上跟陌生人争起来。
a propensity for something = 好某事的倾向
a propensity for doing something = 好做某事的倾向
② The dog showed a propensity to chase anything that moved.
= 那条狗见什么动就爱追什么。
a propensity to do something = 爱做某事的倾向
比 tendency 正式，多指天性。

proper
/ˈprɒpə(r)/
核心：形容词：（英式口语）像样的、真正的、名副其实的，只放在名词前：a proper meal；正确的、恰当的、合适的；
（社会上、道德上）得体的、规矩的、正派的；放在名词后：严格意义上的、本身：the city proper；
（英式口语）十足的、彻底的：a proper fool；proper to 是（正式）为……所特有的。
副词 properly：正确地、恰当地、好好地；得体地；（英式口语）十足地、真正地。习语 properly speaking 是严格说来。并入本条。
（语法）proper noun、proper name 是专有名词。
① We have not had a proper holiday since the children were born.
= 自打孩子出生，我们就没正经度过一次假。
a proper holiday = 像样的假期
a proper meal = 一顿像样的饭
② Please put the books back in their proper place on the shelf.
= 请把书放回书架上原来的位置。
the proper place = 该放的位置
the proper way to do something = 做某事的正确办法
③ It is not considered proper to arrive at a funeral in bright colours.
= 穿着鲜艳的衣服去参加葬礼，是不合规矩的。
it is not proper to do something = 做某事不合规矩
right and proper = 理所应当
④ Her grandmother was a very proper lady who never raised her voice.
= 她奶奶是位规规矩矩的老太太，从不高声说话。
a very proper lady = 规规矩矩的女士
prim and proper = 一本正经
⑤ They live in a village outside the city proper, twenty minutes by bus.
= 他们住在市区以外的一个村子里，坐公共汽车二十分钟。
the city proper = 市区本身
这一支放在名词后面。
⑥ I felt a proper fool standing there in the rain with no umbrella.
= 我没带伞站在雨里，觉得自己真是个大傻瓜。
a proper fool = 十足的傻瓜
这样用是英式口语。
⑦ The teacher showed the dignity proper to her position.
= 那位老师显出了与她的身份相称的庄重。
proper to something = 为某物所特有的、与某物相称的
这一支是正式用法。
⑧ The machine will not work unless it is properly connected.
= 那台机器接得不对就开不动。
properly connected = 接得对
do something properly = 把某事做对、做好
⑨ You cannot eat properly on the money they pay you.
= 靠他们给的那点钱，你吃不上像样的饭。
eat properly = 好好吃饭
sleep properly = 好好睡觉
⑩ He does not know how to behave properly at the table.
= 他不知道在饭桌上该怎么守规矩。
behave properly = 举止得体
dress properly = 穿得得体
⑪ Properly speaking, a tomato is a fruit and not a vegetable.
= 严格说来，番茄是水果，不是蔬菜。
properly speaking = 严格说来
⑫ "London" and "Mary" are proper nouns and begin with a capital letter.
= 「London」和「Mary」是专有名词，首字母大写。
a proper noun = 专有名词
a proper name = 专有名词

property
/ˈprɒpəti/
核心：三支。一是财产、所有物，不可数；二是房地产、不动产，不可数；一处房产，可数，复数 properties；
三是（正式）（物质的）性质、特性，可数，多用复数。
形容词 propertied 是（正式）有产的、拥有地产的，只放在名词前。并入本条。
复合词：property developer 是房地产开发商；lost property 是失物、失物招领处；intellectual property 是知识产权。
① The books are the property of the school and must not be taken home.
= 那些书是学校的财产，不得带回家。
the property of somebody = 某人的财产
personal property = 私人财物
② The police found a van full of stolen property behind the garage.
= 警察在车库后面找到一辆装满赃物的货车。
stolen property = 赃物
这一支不可数。
③ The price of property in the city has doubled in ten years.
= 那座城市的房价十年翻了一番。
the price of property = 房价
property prices = 房价
④ The firm owns several properties in the centre of the town.
= 那家公司在镇中心有好几处房产。
a property = 一处房产
这样用可数。
⑤ The plant has been used for centuries for its healing properties.
= 那种植物因为有疗效，已经用了好几百年。
healing properties = 疗效
the properties of something = 某物的性质
⑥ He made his fortune as a property developer in the nineteen eighties.
= 他是一九八〇年代做房地产开发商发的财。
a property developer = 房地产开发商
⑦ I handed the umbrella in at the lost property office in the station.
= 我把那把伞交到了车站的失物招领处。
lost property = 失物、失物招领处
a lost property office = 失物招领处
⑧ The song is the intellectual property of the woman who wrote it.
= 那首歌是写它的那位女士的知识产权。
intellectual property = 知识产权
⑨ In those days only the propertied classes had the right to vote.
= 那年头只有有产阶级才有选举权。
the propertied classes = 有产阶级
⑩ The parks of the city are public property and belong to everyone.
= 城里的公园是公共财产，属于每一个人。
public property = 公共财产
也指尽人皆知的事。

prophecy
/ˈprɒfəsi/
核心：两支。一是预言，可数，复数 prophecies；二是预言的能力、预言这件事，不可数。名词结尾拼 -cy，读 /si/；动词拼 prophesy。
① The old woman's prophecy that the king would die young came true.
= 老妇人说国王会早死，这个预言应验了。
a prophecy that… = ……的预言
fulfil a prophecy = 应验预言
② The people believed that the priestess had the gift of prophecy.
= 人们相信那位女祭司有预言的本领。
the gift of prophecy = 预言的本领
这一支不可数。
③ Telling a child he will fail can become a self-fulfilling prophecy.
= 对孩子说他会失败，可能真就说成了。
a self-fulfilling prophecy = 自我应验的预言

prophesy
/ˈprɒfəsaɪ/
核心：预言、预告：说出将来会发生的事，尤指凭神力、凭宗教的灵感。及物也可不及物。动词结尾拼 -sy，读 /saɪ/；过去式 prophesied。
① The priest prophesied that a great flood would destroy the city.
= 祭司预言一场大洪水会毁掉那座城。
prophesy that… = 预言……
② Few people would have prophesied her success when she left school at fifteen.
= 她十五岁退学时，没几个人料得到她会成功。
prophesy something = 预言某事
prophesy disaster = 预言灾难
别跟名词 prophecy 弄混：拼法、读音都不一样。

prophet
/ˈprɒfɪt/
核心：三支，可数。一是（宗教里的）先知：传达神的旨意的人；the Prophet 在伊斯兰教里指穆罕默德；二是预言家：说得出将来的事的人；
三是 a prophet of：（一种新主张的）倡导者。女先知叫 prophetess。习语 a prophet of doom 是总说要出事的人、报丧的。并入本条。
① The prophets of the Old Testament warned the people to change their ways.
= 《旧约》里的先知们告诫人们改过。
a prophet = 先知
the Prophet = 先知穆罕默德
② I am no prophet, but I think the firm will be gone within a year.
= 我不是预言家，不过我看那家公司撑不过一年。
be no prophet = 不是预言家
这一支指料得准将来的人。
③ He was an early prophet of the idea that machines could think.
= 他很早就倡导机器能思考这个想法。
a prophet of something = 某种主张的倡导者
④ The prophets of doom said that the bridge would never be finished.
= 那些报丧的说那座桥永远修不完。
a prophet of doom = 总说要出事的人
⑤ In the story a prophetess warns the queen not to cross the river.
= 故事里一位女先知告诫王后不要过那条河。
a prophetess = 女先知

prophetic
/prəˈfetɪk/
核心：两支。一是预言性的、后来应验了的；二是（正式）先知的、像先知的。副词 prophetically，并入本条。
① His warning about the weakness of the dam proved prophetic ten years later.
= 他说那座坝不牢靠，十年后证明是说中了。
prove prophetic = 证明是说中了
prophetic words = 说中了的话
② The old man had a long white beard and a prophetic air.
= 老人留着长长的白胡子，一副先知的派头。
a prophetic air = 先知的派头
这一支是正式用法。
③ "This will end badly," she said prophetically as they set off.
= 他们出发时她说：「这事不会有好结果。」后来果然被她说中。
prophetically = 预言般地、说中地

prophylactic
/ˌprɒfɪˈlæktɪk/
核心：（医学）形容词：预防性的、预防疾病的。名词，可数：预防药、预防措施；（美式，正式）避孕套。
副词 prophylactically；名词 prophylaxis 是预防、预防措施，不可数。并入本条。
① The soldiers were given prophylactic drugs against malaria before they sailed.
= 士兵们上船前领了预防疟疾的药。
prophylactic drugs = 预防药
prophylactic treatment = 预防性治疗
② The tablets are taken as a prophylactic by travellers to the region.
= 去那个地区的旅客把那种药片当预防药吃。
a prophylactic = 预防药
take something as a prophylactic = 把某物当预防药吃
③ The drug is given prophylactically to patients before the operation.
= 手术前给病人用那种药作预防。
prophylactically = 预防性地
④ Regular brushing is the simplest form of dental prophylaxis.
= 按时刷牙是最简单的护牙预防办法。
prophylaxis = 预防
dental prophylaxis = 牙病预防

propinquity
/prəˈpɪŋkwəti/
核心：（正式）（地点、时间上的）邻近、接近；也指（关系上的）亲近、近亲关系。不可数。
① The two families were brought together by the propinquity of their farms.
= 两家的农场挨着，这把两家人拉到了一起。
the propinquity of something = 某物的邻近
propinquity = 邻近
② Friendships at college often grow out of mere propinquity.
= 大学里的友谊往往只是因为住得近才生出来的。
mere propinquity = 只是离得近
日常说 nearness、closeness。

propitiate
/prəˈpɪʃieɪt/
核心：（正式）安抚、劝解、使息怒：做点什么让生气的人、神不再生气。及物。
名词 propitiation，不可数；形容词 propitiatory 是劝解的、求和的。并入本条。
① The villagers offered a goat to propitiate the god of the mountain.
= 村民献上一只山羊，求山神息怒。
propitiate a god = 求神息怒
propitiate somebody = 安抚某人
② He sent flowers in an attempt to propitiate his angry mother-in-law.
= 他送了花，想让生气的岳母消消气。
an attempt to propitiate somebody = 想安抚某人
日常说 appease、placate。
③ The animals were killed as an act of propitiation before the battle.
= 开战之前，人们宰了牲畜祭神求和。
an act of propitiation = 求神息怒的举动
④ She gave him a propitiatory smile and offered to make the tea.
= 她冲他赔了个笑脸，说她来沏茶。
a propitiatory smile = 赔笑
propitiatory = 劝解的、求和的

propitious
/prəˈpɪʃəs/
核心：（正式）吉利的、有利的、适宜的：propitious for。反义 unpropitious。
① It was not a propitious time to ask the bank for another loan.
= 那不是向银行再借一笔钱的好时机。
a propitious time = 好时机
a propitious moment = 有利的时刻
② The calm weather was propitious for the crossing to the island.
= 风平浪静的天气利于渡海上岛。
be propitious for something = 利于某事
日常说 favourable。

proponent
/prəˈpəʊnənt/
核心：（正式）倡导者、支持者、拥护者：公开主张一种想法、做法的人。可数。常说 a proponent of。反义 opponent。
① She is a leading proponent of teaching music in every primary school.
= 她是主张每所小学都教音乐的带头人。
a proponent of something = 某事的倡导者
a leading proponent = 带头倡导的人
② Proponents of the scheme say that it will create two thousand jobs.
= 支持那个方案的人说它会带来两千个工作岗位。
proponents of the scheme = 支持那个方案的人
跟 advocate 意思相近。

proportion
/prəˈpɔːʃn/
核心：名词：（整体里的）部分、份额，可数：a large proportion of；比例、比：甲与乙在数量、大小上的关系，可数也可不可数：the proportion of A to B；
匀称、相称，不可数，也用复数；proportions 是（很大的）规模、尺寸、程度；（数学）比例，不可数。
习语：in proportion to 是与……成比例、相对于……来说；in proportion 是比例合适，也指看得恰如其分；
out of proportion 是不成比例、过分；out of all proportion to 是跟……完全不相称；
keep something in proportion 是恰如其分地看待；a sense of proportion 是分寸感、分得清轻重。
派生：proportional、proportionate 是成比例的、相称的，副词 proportionally、proportionately；proportionality 是（正式）相称、比例原则，不可数；
-proportioned 构成形容词：well-proportioned 是匀称的。并入本条。
① A large proportion of the students come from villages in the north.
= 很大一部分学生来自北方的村子。
a large proportion of something = 某物的很大一部分
a small proportion = 一小部分
② The proportion of women to men in the profession has risen steadily.
= 那一行里女性对男性的比例稳步上升。
the proportion of A to B = 甲对乙的比例
the proportion of women = 女性所占的比例
③ Mix the sand and the cement in the proportion of three to one.
= 把沙子和水泥按三比一的比例拌在一起。
in the proportion of three to one = 按三比一的比例
in equal proportions = 等量地
④ The architect admired the fine proportions of the old town hall.
= 建筑师很欣赏老市政厅优美匀称的比例。
fine proportions = 优美匀称的比例
这一支多用复数。
⑤ The crisis has now reached alarming proportions in the cities of the south.
= 那场危机在南方的城市里已经到了惊人的程度。
reach alarming proportions = 到了惊人的程度
of epic proportions = 规模极大的
⑥ The fee you pay is in proportion to the size of your house.
= 你交的费用跟你房子的大小成比例。
in proportion to = 与……成比例
in proportion to something = 相对于某物来说
⑦ The head of the figure in the drawing is not in proportion to the body.
= 那幅画里人物的头跟身子不成比例。
be in proportion = 比例合适
not in proportion = 不成比例
⑧ The newspapers have blown the whole affair out of proportion.
= 报纸把整件事夸大得不像话。
out of proportion = 不成比例、过分
blow something out of proportion = 把某事夸大
⑨ The punishment was out of all proportion to the seriousness of the offence.
= 那个处罚跟过错的轻重完全不相称。
out of all proportion = 完全不相称
out of all proportion to something = 跟某事完全不相称
⑩ Try to keep the problem in proportion, for nobody has been hurt.
= 这个问题别看得太重，毕竟没人受伤。
keep something in proportion = 恰如其分地看待某事
反过来是 get something out of proportion。
⑪ He has lost all sense of proportion since the argument with his neighbour.
= 自打跟邻居吵了那一架，他就完全分不清轻重了。
a sense of proportion = 分寸感
lose all sense of proportion = 完全分不清轻重
⑫ The pay is proportional to the number of hours you have worked.
= 报酬跟你干的钟点数成正比。
be proportional to something = 与某物成比例
directly proportional = 成正比
⑬ Small firms spend proportionally more on rent than large ones do.
= 按比例算，小公司花在房租上的钱比大公司多。
proportionally = 按比例地
proportionally more = 按比例算更多
⑭ The court held that the fine was not proportionate to the damage done.
= 法院认定那笔罚款跟造成的损害不相称。
be proportionate to something = 与某物相称
反义是 disproportionate。
⑮ Prices have risen, but wages have not increased proportionately.
= 物价涨了，工资却没有相应地涨。
proportionately = 相应地、成比例地
⑯ The judges considered the proportionality of the sentence to the crime.
= 法官们斟酌了量刑与罪行是否相称。
proportionality = 相称、比例原则
这是法律、正式文体里的词。
⑰ It is a well-proportioned room with tall windows on two sides.
= 那是一间比例匀称的屋子，两面都有高窗。
well-proportioned = 匀称的
badly proportioned = 比例不好的

proposal
/prəˈpəʊzl/
核心：两支，可数。一是提议、建议、方案：a proposal for、a proposal to do、a proposal that；二是求婚。也作不可数，指「提出」这件事。
① The council has rejected a proposal to build flats on the playing field.
= 镇议会否决了在操场上盖公寓的提议。
a proposal to do something = 做某事的提议
reject a proposal = 否决提议
② The minister put forward proposals for reforming the tax system.
= 部长提出了改革税制的方案。
proposals for something = 关于某事的方案
put forward a proposal = 提出方案
③ She accepted his proposal of marriage on the spot.
= 她当场答应了他的求婚。
a proposal of marriage = 求婚
accept a proposal = 答应求婚

propose
/prəˈpəʊz/
核心：动词。一是提议、建议：propose something、propose doing、propose that；二是（正式）打算、计划：propose to do、propose doing；
三是求婚：propose (to somebody)；四是（正式）提名、推举：propose somebody for、as；在会上提出（动议）；
五是（正式）提出（一种理论、解释）供人考虑。习语 propose a toast (to somebody) 是提议（为某人）干杯。提议人、动议人叫 proposer。并入本条。
① The committee proposed a number of changes to the rules of the club.
= 委员会对俱乐部的章程提出了几处修改。
propose a change = 提出修改
propose something = 提议某事
② She proposed that the meeting should be put off until Monday.
= 她提议把会推到星期一。
propose that… = 提议……
propose doing something = 提议做某事
③ How do you propose to pay for a car on your wages?
= 凭你那点工资，你打算怎么买车？
propose to do something = 打算做某事
这一支是正式说法，问话时常带怀疑。
④ He proposed to her on the beach where they had first met.
= 他在两人初次相遇的那片海滩上向她求了婚。
propose to somebody = 向某人求婚
propose marriage = 求婚
⑤ I should like to propose Mrs Harris for the post of treasurer.
= 我想提名哈里斯太太当司库。
propose somebody for something = 提名某人任某职
propose somebody as something = 推举某人当某职
⑥ The scientist proposed a new explanation for the sudden rise in temperature.
= 那位科学家对气温骤升提出了一种新解释。
propose an explanation = 提出一种解释
propose a theory = 提出一种理论
⑦ The best man stood up and proposed a toast to the bride and groom.
= 伴郎站起来，提议为新娘新郎干杯。
propose a toast = 提议干杯
propose a toast to somebody = 提议为某人干杯
⑧ The proposer of the motion spoke for ten minutes in its favour.
= 动议的提出人替它讲了十分钟。
the proposer of a motion = 动议的提出人
a proposer = 提议人

proposition
/ˌprɒpəˈzɪʃn/
核心：名词，可数：（生意上的）提议、建议；（要对付的）事情、问题：a different proposition；（正式）主张、论点、命题；
（美国）（交给公民投票的）法律提案；（数学）命题。动词：向（人）提出性要求，尤指唐突、冒犯地。
形容词 propositional 是命题的。并入本条。
① I have a business proposition that I should like to put to you.
= 我有个生意上的提议想跟你谈谈。
a business proposition = 生意上的提议
put a proposition to somebody = 向某人提出建议
② Climbing the mountain in winter is a very different proposition.
= 冬天爬那座山可完全是另一回事。
a different proposition = 另一回事
a tough proposition = 难对付的事
③ The book defends the proposition that all people are born equal.
= 那本书为「人人生而平等」这个主张辩护。
the proposition that… = ……这个主张
这一支是正式用法。
④ Voters in the state rejected the proposition by a narrow margin.
= 那个州的选民以微弱多数否决了那项提案。
a proposition = （付公民投票的）提案
这是美国的说法。
⑤ She was propositioned by a stranger in the bar of the hotel.
= 她在旅馆的酒吧里遭到一个陌生人的非礼搭讪。
proposition somebody = 向某人提出性要求
这样用带冒犯、唐突的意思。
⑥ The course begins with the rules of propositional logic.
= 那门课从命题逻辑的规则讲起。
propositional logic = 命题逻辑
propositional = 命题的

propound
/prəˈpaʊnd/
核心：（正式）提出（一种理论、主张）供人考虑、讨论。及物。
① The theory was first propounded by a Greek thinker two thousand years ago.
= 那个理论最早是两千年前一位希腊思想家提出的。
propound a theory = 提出一种理论
be propounded by somebody = 由某人提出
② He used the lecture to propound his own views on education.
= 他借那场讲座宣讲自己对教育的看法。
propound your views = 提出自己的看法
日常说 put forward。

proprietary
/prəˈpraɪətri/
核心：三支，多放在名词前。一是（商品）专卖的、有商标的、某家公司专有的；二是所有人的、所有权的；三是俨然以主人自居的。
① The chemist sells proprietary medicines as well as cheaper unbranded ones.
= 那家药店卖有商标的成药，也卖便宜的无牌药。
proprietary medicines = 有商标的成药
a proprietary brand = 专卖的牌子
② The firm claims proprietary rights over the design of the engine.
= 那家公司声称对那台发动机的设计拥有所有权。
proprietary rights = 所有权
proprietary software = 专有软件
③ He laid a proprietary hand on her shoulder as they came in.
= 两人进来时，他俨然以主人自居地把手搭在她肩上。
a proprietary hand = 俨然占有的手势
a proprietary air = 以主人自居的派头

proprietor
/prəˈpraɪətə(r)/
核心：（正式）（商店、旅馆、报纸的）业主、所有人、老板。可数。女业主旧时叫 proprietress。
形容词 proprietorial 是所有人的、以主人自居的，副词 proprietorially；proprietorship 是所有权，不可数。并入本条。
① The proprietor of the hotel came out to welcome us himself.
= 旅馆老板亲自出来迎接我们。
the proprietor of a hotel = 旅馆的老板
a newspaper proprietor = 报业老板
② Complaints should be addressed to the proprietor in writing.
= 有意见请书面向业主提出。
the proprietor = 业主
比 owner 正式。
③ The proprietress of the tea shop knew every customer by name.
= 茶室的女老板叫得出每一位顾客的名字。
a proprietress = 女业主
这个词如今显得老派。
④ She watched the builders with a proprietorial eye from the upstairs window.
= 她从楼上的窗口以主人的眼光盯着那些建筑工人。
a proprietorial eye = 主人的眼光
proprietorial rights = 所有权
⑤ The cat stretched out proprietorially on the visitor's coat in the hall.
= 那只猫大模大样地摊在客人放在门厅的大衣上，仿佛那是它的。
proprietorially = 以主人自居地
⑥ The paper changed its politics under the proprietorship of the new owner.
= 在新老板名下，那份报纸改了政治立场。
proprietorship = 所有权
under somebody's proprietorship = 在某人名下

propriety
/prəˈpraɪəti/
核心：（正式）两支。一是得体、合乎规矩、行为正当，不可数；二是 the proprieties：礼节、规矩，复数。反义 impropriety。
① Nobody questioned the propriety of her being alone with him in the house.
= 她跟他单独待在那栋房子里合不合适，没有人提出疑问。
the propriety of something = 某事合不合适
question the propriety of something = 质疑某事是否得当
② He always behaved with the utmost propriety towards his pupils.
= 他对学生的举止向来极其规矩。
with propriety = 规规矩矩地
the utmost propriety = 极其规矩
③ The family were careful to observe the proprieties at the funeral.
= 那家人在葬礼上处处留心礼数。
the proprieties = 礼节、规矩
observe the proprieties = 守礼数

propulsion
/prəˈpʌlʃn/
核心：（术语）推进、推进力：把车、船、飞机往前推的力。不可数。形容词 propulsive 是推进的、有推动力的。并入本条。
① The submarine uses nuclear propulsion and can stay under water for months.
= 那艘潜艇用核动力推进，能在水下待好几个月。
nuclear propulsion = 核动力推进
jet propulsion = 喷气推进
② The engineers are testing a new propulsion system for the rocket.
= 工程师们在测试那枚火箭的一套新推进系统。
a propulsion system = 推进系统
这一支不可数。
③ The fish gets its propulsive force from the sweep of its tail.
= 鱼靠摆尾巴得到往前的推力。
propulsive force = 推力
propulsive = 推进的

prorate
/ˌprəʊˈreɪt/
核心：（美式）按比例分配、按比例计算（费用、报酬）。及物，多用被动。
① The rent is prorated if you move in after the first of the month.
= 月初以后搬进来的，房租按天数比例算。
be prorated = 按比例算
prorate the rent = 按比例算房租
② Her salary was prorated because she worked only three days a week.
= 她一个星期只上三天班，薪水是按比例折算的。
a prorated salary = 按比例折算的薪水
英式多说 pro rata。

prorogue
/prəˈrəʊɡ/
核心：（正式，术语）让（议会）休会、闭会：不解散议会，只把会期暂时停掉。及物，多用被动。
① Parliament was prorogued for five weeks at the request of the Prime Minister.
= 应首相之请，议会休会五个星期。
be prorogued = 休会
prorogue Parliament = 让议会休会
② The decision to prorogue the assembly was challenged in the courts.
= 让议会休会的决定在法院受到了质疑。
a decision to prorogue = 休会的决定
跟 dissolve（解散）不一样：休会之后还是原来那届议会。

prosaic
/prəˈzeɪɪk/
核心：（多含贬义）两支。一是平淡的、乏味的、没有想象力的；二是平凡的、实实在在的、不带浪漫色彩的。副词 prosaically，并入本条。
① He writes in a flat, prosaic style that sends the reader to sleep.
= 他的文笔平板乏味，读得人直犯困。
a prosaic style = 乏味的文笔
a prosaic mind = 没有想象力的头脑
② The truth behind the legend turned out to be rather more prosaic.
= 那个传说背后的真相原来平淡得多。
be more prosaic = 平淡得多
a prosaic explanation = 平淡无奇的解释
③ "It is only the wind in the chimney," she said prosaically.
= 「不过是烟囱里的风。」她平平淡淡地说。
prosaically = 平淡地

proscenium
/prəˈsiːniəm/
核心：（剧场）舞台口：舞台上大幕以前的那一部分。可数。proscenium arch 是舞台口的拱形框：把舞台和观众席隔开的那道框。
① The actor stepped forward on to the proscenium to speak to the audience.
= 那位演员走到舞台口，对观众说话。
the proscenium = 舞台口
on the proscenium = 在舞台口
② The gilded proscenium arch frames the stage like an enormous picture.
= 镀金的台口拱框把舞台框得像一幅巨大的画。
a proscenium arch = 舞台口的拱框
指老式剧场里把舞台和观众席分开的那道框。

prosciutto
/prəˈʃuːtəʊ/
核心：意大利熏火腿：一种风干的生火腿，切成极薄的片吃。不可数。
① The starter was slices of melon wrapped in prosciutto.
= 头盘是裹着意大利火腿的甜瓜片。
prosciutto = 意大利熏火腿
wrapped in prosciutto = 裹着意大利火腿
② She bought a hundred grams of prosciutto from the Italian shop on the corner.
= 她在街角那家意大利店买了一百克意大利火腿。
a slice of prosciutto = 一片意大利火腿
是意大利语借词，sci 读 /ʃ/。

proscribe
/prəˈskraɪb/
核心：（正式）（官方）禁止、取缔、宣布为非法。及物，多用被动。名词 proscription，可数也可不可数。并入本条。别跟 prescribe（开药、规定）混。
① The organization was proscribed by the government after the bombing.
= 爆炸事件之后，那个组织被政府取缔了。
be proscribed = 被取缔
a proscribed organization = 被取缔的组织
② The use of the drug in sport is proscribed by international rules.
= 国际规则禁止在体育比赛里使用那种药。
proscribe the use of something = 禁止使用某物
跟 prescribe 只差一个字母，意思几乎相反。
③ The proscription of the party drove its members underground.
= 那个党被取缔，党员只好转入地下。
the proscription of something = 对某物的取缔
a proscription against something = 对某事的禁令

prose
/prəʊz/
核心：散文、白话文：不是诗的、平常的书面文字。不可数。常说 in prose。
① The story is told partly in prose and partly in verse.
= 那个故事一部分用散文讲，一部分用诗讲。
in prose = 用散文
prose and verse = 散文和诗
② She writes clear, simple prose that a child could follow.
= 她写的文字清楚简单，小孩子都看得懂。
clear prose = 清楚的文字
a prose style = 文风
③ He is better known for his prose than for his poems.
= 他的散文比他的诗出名。
his prose = 他的散文
a prose writer = 散文作家

prosecute
/ˈprɒsɪkjuːt/
核心：三支。一是起诉、检控：prosecute somebody for，及物也可不及物；二是（在法庭上）担任控方律师；三是（正式）继续进行、把……干到底：prosecute a war。
① The firm was prosecuted for dumping waste in the river.
= 那家公司因为往河里倒废料被起诉了。
be prosecuted for something = 因某事被起诉
prosecute somebody = 起诉某人
② Trespassers will be prosecuted, said a sign on the gate.
= 院门上的牌子写着：擅入者将被起诉。
Trespassers will be prosecuted = 擅入者将被起诉
这是告示上的固定说法。
③ The lawyer who prosecuted the case is now a judge.
= 当年在那桩案子里担任控方的律师如今是法官了。
prosecute a case = 担任一桩案子的控方
the prosecuting counsel = 控方律师
④ The government was determined to prosecute the war to the end.
= 政府决心把那场战争打到底。
prosecute a war = 把仗打下去
这一支是正式用法。

prosecution
/ˌprɒsɪˈkjuːʃn/
核心：三支。一是起诉、检控，可数也可不可数；二是 the prosecution：控方、原告及其律师，单数，动词单复数都行，对着说的是 the defence；
三是（正式）进行、执行，不可数：the prosecution of the war。
① He could face prosecution for driving without insurance.
= 他无保险驾车，可能会被起诉。
face prosecution = 面临起诉
a prosecution for something = 因某事的起诉
② The police have brought a prosecution against the owner of the dog.
= 警方对那条狗的主人提起了公诉。
bring a prosecution = 提起公诉
这样用可数。
③ The prosecution called three witnesses on the first day of the trial.
= 开庭头一天，控方传了三名证人。
the prosecution = 控方
a witness for the prosecution = 控方证人
④ The general was praised for his energetic prosecution of the campaign.
= 那位将军把那场战役打得雷厉风行，受到了称赞。
the prosecution of something = 某事的进行
这一支是正式用法。

prosecutor
/ˈprɒsɪkjuːtə(r)/
核心：两支，可数。一是检察官、公诉人：代表国家起诉人的官员；二是（在法庭上的）控方律师。
① The public prosecutor decided that there was not enough evidence for a trial.
= 检察官认定证据不足，不够开庭。
a public prosecutor = 检察官
a state prosecutor = 州检察官
② The prosecutor asked the witness to describe what she had seen.
= 控方律师请证人讲讲她看见了什么。
the prosecutor = 控方律师
对着说的是 defence lawyer（辩护律师）。

proselytize
/ˈprɒsələtaɪz/
核心：（正式，多含贬义）劝人改信（自己的宗教、主张）、传教。不及物也可及物。英式也拼作 proselytise。
① The group was accused of proselytizing among the students at the college.
= 那个团体被指在学院的学生当中拉人入教。
proselytize among somebody = 在某些人当中劝人改信
proselytize = 劝人改信
② He never tried to proselytize, though his own faith was strong.
= 他自己信得很深，却从不劝别人信。
try to proselytize = 想劝人改信
带贬义：硬拉人入伙。

prosody
/ˈprɒsədi/
核心：（术语）两支，不可数。一是诗体学、韵律学：诗里的节奏和音的格式，以及研究它的学问；二是（语音学）韵律：说话时的重音、节奏和语调。
形容词 prosodic，并入本条。
① The first chapter explains the prosody of Latin verse.
= 第一章讲拉丁诗的韵律。
the prosody of verse = 诗的韵律
prosody = 诗体学、韵律学
② Children pick up the prosody of their language before they learn any words.
= 孩子还没学会一个词，就先学到了母语的语调节奏。
the prosody of a language = 一种语言的韵律
这是语音学里的用法。
③ Stress and pitch are the main prosodic features of English speech.
= 重音和音高是英语口语里主要的韵律特征。
prosodic features = 韵律特征
prosodic = 韵律的

prospect
/ˈprɒspekt/（名）/prəˈspekt/（动）
核心：名词：可能性、希望，不可数或单数：prospect of、prospect that；（对将来的）设想、要来的事、前景，单数：the prospect of；
prospects 是（成功的）前途、前程，复数；有希望的人选、可能的主顾，可数；（正式）（从高处看到的）景色，可数。
动词，英式重音在后：prospect (for)：勘探、找（矿、金子、石油）。习语 in prospect 是（正式）可望发生的、在望。
① There is little prospect of the weather improving before the weekend.
= 周末以前天气好转的希望很小。
little prospect of something = 某事的希望很小
no prospect of doing something = 没有做某事的希望
② There is a real prospect that the factory will close next year.
= 那家工厂明年真有可能关门。
a prospect that… = ……的可能
a real prospect = 实实在在的可能
③ The prospect of a whole summer at the seaside filled the children with joy.
= 想到要在海边过整整一个夏天，孩子们高兴极了。
the prospect of something = 对某事的设想、要来的某事
an exciting prospect = 叫人兴奋的前景
④ She did not relish the prospect of telling her father the news.
= 想到要把那个消息告诉父亲，她心里发怵。
the prospect of doing something = 要做某事这件事
relish the prospect = 乐意想到要来的事
⑤ The job pays badly, but the prospects for promotion are excellent.
= 那份工作钱少，可是升职的前景极好。
the prospects for something = 某事的前景
job prospects = 就业前景
⑥ He is a young man with good prospects and a place at a famous firm.
= 他是个前程不错的年轻人，在一家有名的公司里有位子。
good prospects = 好前程
这一支用复数。
⑦ The young goalkeeper is one of the brightest prospects in the country.
= 那位年轻门将是国内最被看好的新秀之一。
a bright prospect = 被看好的新秀
a prospect = 有希望的人选、可能的主顾
⑧ From the top of the tower there is a fine prospect of the whole valley.
= 从塔顶望去，整个山谷的景色尽收眼底。
a fine prospect = 优美的景色
这一支是正式、文学的用法。
⑨ A further rise in prices is in prospect before the end of the year.
= 年底以前物价可望再涨一次。
in prospect = 在望、可望发生
这是正式说法。
⑩ Thousands of men went north to prospect for gold in the frozen rivers.
= 成千上万的男人北上，到结冰的河里淘金。
prospect for gold = 找金子
prospect for oil = 勘探石油

prospective
/prəˈspektɪv/
核心：两支，多放在名词前。一是可能成为……的、未来的：a prospective buyer；二是预期的、即将发生的。
① The agent showed three prospective buyers round the house on Saturday.
= 星期六中介带了三位可能的买主看那栋房子。
a prospective buyer = 可能的买主
a prospective employer = 未来的雇主
② The college holds an open day for prospective students every spring.
= 那所学院每年春天给有意报考的学生办开放日。
prospective students = 有意报考的学生
指还没成、有可能成的。
③ The union is worried about the prospective changes in the law.
= 工会担心即将到来的法律变动。
prospective changes = 即将到来的变动
别跟 perspective（看法、透视）混。

prospector
/prəˈspektə(r)/
核心：勘探者、探矿人：到处找金子、石油、矿石的人。可数。
① A prospector found gold in the creek, and a town grew up within a year.
= 一个探矿人在小溪里找到了金子，不出一年那里就起了一座城。
a prospector = 探矿人
a gold prospector = 淘金人
② The old prospector had spent forty years in the hills with a mule.
= 那个老探矿人牵着一头骡子在山里过了四十年。
an old prospector = 老探矿人
an oil prospector = 找石油的人

prospectus
/prəˈspektəs/
核心：两支，可数，复数 prospectuses。一是（学校、大学的）简章；二是（公司发股票时给投资人看的）招股说明书。
① I have sent off for the prospectus of the art college in Leeds.
= 我去函索要了利兹那所艺术学院的简章。
a college prospectus = 学院简章
the school prospectus = 学校简章
② The firm must publish a prospectus before it sells shares to the public.
= 那家公司向公众卖股票之前，必须公布招股说明书。
publish a prospectus = 公布招股说明书
a prospectus = 招股说明书

prosper
/ˈprɒspə(r)/
核心：兴旺、发达、成功：尤指在钱财、生意上。不及物。
① The town prospered in the years when wool fetched a high price.
= 羊毛卖得出高价的那些年，那座城很兴旺。
a town prospers = 一座城兴旺
prosper = 兴旺、发达
② She moved to the city, opened a small bakery and prospered.
= 她搬到城里，开了家小面包店，生意兴隆。
a business prospers = 生意兴隆
多指钱财上的顺利。

prosperity
/prɒˈsperəti/
核心：繁荣、富足、兴旺：尤指钱财上的宽裕。不可数。
① The discovery of oil brought prosperity to the whole region.
= 发现石油给整个地区带来了繁荣。
bring prosperity = 带来繁荣
economic prosperity = 经济繁荣
② The country enjoyed a long period of peace and prosperity after the war.
= 战后那个国家过了很长一段太平富足的日子。
peace and prosperity = 太平富足
a period of prosperity = 一段繁荣的时期

prosperous
/ˈprɒspərəs/
核心：繁荣的、富裕的、兴旺的：有钱而且顺利。比 rich 正式，也更含蓄。
① He was the son of a prosperous farmer in the west of the country.
= 他是国内西部一个殷实农户的儿子。
a prosperous farmer = 殷实的农户
a prosperous family = 富裕的人家
② It is one of the most prosperous towns in the south of the country.
= 那是南方最富庶的城镇之一。
a prosperous town = 富庶的城镇
a prosperous country = 富裕的国家

prostate
/ˈprɒsteɪt/
核心：前列腺：男性膀胱下面的一个腺体，分泌精液里的液体。可数。也说 prostate gland。别跟 prostrate（俯卧的）混。
① The risk of cancer of the prostate rises sharply after the age of sixty.
= 六十岁以后，得前列腺癌的风险急剧上升。
cancer of the prostate = 前列腺癌
